We conducted extensive experiments across models demonstrating that at 50\% sparsity, AoH retains 96.5\% of Full Attention performance on average while reducing prefill and decode latency by up to 41.4\% and 66.0\%, respectively, and KV-cache memory by 50.0\% at 256K tokens.

</details>


### [99] [LLMRouter: Unified Infrastructure for Developing, Evaluating, and Deploying LLM Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06867)
*Tao Feng,Fangxu Yu,Haozhen Zhang,Zhongjie Dai,Liangqi Yuan,Zijie Lei,Weizhi Zhang,Kunlun Zhu,Haodong Yue,Keyang Xuan,Ge Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将LLM路由统一建模为序列决策过程的框架，涵盖五个核心组件，并据此构建了自动化评估基准xRouteBench和开源模块化基础设施LLMRouter。实证表明，学习型路由器相比最强固定模型基线相对提升14.6%，且在成本约束下轻量路由器更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一LLM无法在所有查询和预算约束下都表现最优，因此模型路由（model routing）对成本效益部署至关重要。然而，现有路由器采用多样化的公式和实现方式，缺乏统一框架，导致公平比较和扩展困难。

Method: 首先，将LLM路由统一表述为序列决策过程，定义五个核心组件：上下文编码器（context encoders）、模型编码器（model encoders）、评分函数（scoring functions）、决策规则（decision rules）和学习信号（learning signals）。该框架覆盖单轮、多轮及个性化路由。在此基础上，开发了自动化流水线用于构建路由监督信号，并联合评估路由器的响应质量与推理成本。进而构建了基准xRouteBench，涵盖通用LLM、记忆增强、视觉、时间序列和个性化路由任务。同时发布了开源模块化基础设施LLMRouter，集成超过16种代表性路由器。

Result: 学习型路由器相比最强固定模型基线在响应质量上相对提升14.6%；在严格成本约束条件下，轻量级路由器表现更具竞争力；用户条件化的路由策略持续改善个性化表现。

Conclusion: 统一的序列决策框架有效整合了各类路由方法，xRouteBench为公平评估提供了标准化平台。学习型路由策略在质量-成本权衡上显著优于固定模型选择，轻量路由器和个性化路由在不同场景下展现出明显优势。

Abstract: No single large language model (LLM) is optimal across all queries and budget constraints, making model routing essential for cost-effective deployment. Existing routers adopt diverse formulations and implementations, making fair comparison and extension difficult. We present a unified formulation of LLM routing as a sequential decision process characterized by five components: context encoders, model encoders, scoring functions, decision rules, and learning signals, covering single-turn, multi-turn, and personalized routing. Based on this formulation, we develop an automated pipeline for constructing routing supervision and evaluating routers jointly on response quality and inference cost. The resulting benchmark, xRouteBench, spans generic LLM, memory-augmented, vision, time-series, and personalized routing tasks. We further introduce LLMRouter, an open-source modular infrastructure with more than 16 representative routers. Our empirical study shows that learned routers outperform the strongest fixed-model baseline by 14.6% relatively, lightweight routers become more competitive under tight cost constraints, and user-conditioned routing consistently improves personalization.

</details>


### [100] [Georeferencing Non-Gazetteered Place Names using Biological Specimen Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06884)
*Aneesha Fernando,Surangika Ranathunga,Kristin Stock,Raj Prasanna,Christopher B. Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用新西兰Allan Herbarium数字化标本记录，识别出当前地名录中缺失的历史/方言地名（非地名录地名，NGP），并提出基于确定性、概率和大语言模型三种方法，通过跨标本记录的重复出现和空间关系约束来推断NGP的地理坐标。在伪NGP基准测试中，概率推理精度最高（中位误差1.43 km），LLM次之（1.80 km），表明高空间精度需求下传统建模仍具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然历史标本记录蕴含丰富的历史地理知识，但许多描述采集地点的历史、方言或口语化地名已不在现行地名录中（即NGP），导致这些标本无法有效地理参考。传统方法难以仅凭标本记录中有限信息推断这些地名的空间位置，亟需一种能利用跨记录空间关系约束的推理方法。

Method: 首先从Allan Herbarium数字化标本的采集地描述中识别NGP；然后利用同一地名在不同标本记录中的重复出现，以及N、S、E、W、near等空间关系词，提取并反转空间关系，构建NGP位置的约束条件；在此基础上分别采用确定性推理、概率推理和基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法进行空间推断，并在伪NGP基准上进行对比评估。

Result: 概率推理表现最佳（中位误差1.43 km，A@1 km达36%），LLM方法竞争力强但精度略逊（中位误差1.80 km，A@1 km为31%），确定性方法结果未详述但可作为基线。

Conclusion: 尽管大语言模型在文本空间推理上展现出竞争力，但在高空间精度需求场景中，传统概率建模方法仍优于LLM。利用跨标本记录的空间关系约束进行NGP地理参考是一种有效策略。

Abstract: Biological specimen records collected by natural history institutions constitute a rich source of temporal geographic knowledge, capturing biodiversity information about regional landscapes as they were recorded at different times. Using digitised data from the Allan Herbarium (New Zealand), this study identifies place names in these specimen locality descriptions that are absent from current gazetteers; we refer to these as non-gazetteer place names (NGPs). These place names are typically historical, vernacular, or colloquial and were used as landmarks to describe a specimen's location at the time of collection. We then investigate the problem of georeferencing the NGPs using only the limited information available in the specimen records. To resolve this, we leverage repeated occurrences of the same place name across specimen records with different specimen locations and spatial relation terms, extracting and inverting these relations to derive constraints on NGP locations. This approach is instantiated within deterministic, probabilistic, and LLM-based methods, enabling a comparative analysis of their strengths and limitations for text-based spatial inference. On a pseudo-NGP benchmark, probabilistic inference achieves the highest accuracy (median error 1.43 km; A@1 km 36%), while the LLM yields competitive but less precise estimates (median error 1.80 km; A@1 km 31%), indicating that, despite advances in LLMs, traditional modelling remains advantageous when high spatial precision is required.

</details>


### [101] [Calibrating WEAT Against Anisotropy: ZCA Whitening as a Geometric Pre-Processing Step for Embedding Association Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06908)
*Seitaro Ono,Senna Ross,Jun Saiki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将ZCA白化作为WEAT的几何预处理步骤，以恢复嵌入空间的各向同性条件。实验表明该方法显著降低各向异性，超过30%的WEAT结果显著性发生改变，提示此前在非各向同性空间中的偏差测量需谨慎解读并重新评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WEAT依赖余弦相似度测量语义关联，其前提是嵌入空间近似各向同性。但许多广泛使用的语言模型不满足该假设，导致偏差测量的可靠性存疑。因此需要一种预处理方法来恢复各向同性条件。

Method: 采用ZCA白化作为几何预处理方法，将嵌入空间的协方差矩阵变换为单位矩阵，同时在最小化对原始向量扰动的前提下恢复各向同性。在10个标准WEAT测试套件、3个架构家族的7个模型（共70个模型-任务组合）上进行评估。

Result: ZCA白化显著降低了所有模型的嵌入空间各向异性；对高各向异性模型，在标准语义相似度基准上也有提升。校准后超过30%的WEAT结果显著性状态发生变化，效应量根据偏差类别双向变动。

Conclusion: 此前在非各向同性嵌入空间中报告的偏差测量结果应谨慎解读，并可能需用校准方法重新评估。该方法有助于恢复WEAT在计算社会科学和AI公平性研究中的测量基础。

Abstract: We propose Zero-phase Component Analysis (ZCA) whitening as a geometric pre-processing step for the Word Embedding Association Test (WEAT). WEAT is a bias measurement method widely used in both computational social science and AI fairness research. It relies on cosine similarity as a measure of semantic association, which assumes that the embedding space is approximately isotropic. However, prior work has reported that many widely used language models do not satisfy this assumption, raising concerns about the reliability of bias measurements. ZCA whitening transforms the covariance of the embedding space into the identity matrix while minimizing perturbation to the original vectors. This transformation restores the isotropy condition on which WEAT relies. We evaluate our approach on ten standard WEAT test suites and seven models spanning three architectural families, yielding 70 model-task combinations. The results show that ZCA whitening substantially reduces the anisotropy of the embedding spaces across all models. Particularly for highly anisotropic models, we further observe improvements on standard semantic similarity benchmarks, indicating that the calibrated space better captures semantic associations. After calibration, over 30% of WEAT results change significance status, and effect sizes shift in both directions depending on bias category. These shifts suggest that uncalibrated measurements may both overestimate and underestimate the associations encoded in the embedding space. These findings indicate that previously reported bias measurements in anisotropic embedding spaces should be interpreted with caution and may benefit from re-evaluation with calibrated methods. Our approach contributes to restoring the measurement foundation of WEAT across both computational social science and AI fairness research.

</details>


### [102] [Ask-E: An Environment for Calibrated Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06933)
*Sarah Pratt,Jae Sung Park,Scott Geng,Ali Farhadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Ask-E 环境，通过让模型生成能精准区分两个模型能力边界的问题（即恰好一个能解、一个不能解）来基准测试和训练模型，而非让模型回答问题。前沿模型校准率不足50%，且在该环境训练后可提升下游数学基准，无需新数据或更强模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿模型评估与训练依赖人工设计高难度问题，随着模型能力提升，生成精准校准于模型能力边界的问题变得越来越困难。本文的核心洞察是将这一约束转化为优势：若能持续生成精准校准于某一能力前沿的问题，则说明生成模型本身的能力已超越该前沿。

Method: 提出 Ask-E 环境：定义目标技能等级为由两个已有语言模型能力所界定的区间。一个问题若恰好能被其中一个模型解决而另一个不能，则视为成功校准，表明该问题精确落于目标区间内并有效区分两模型能力。Ask-E 同时作为基准测试和训练环境，模型需针对多种技能等级生成校准问题。

Result: 前沿模型在 Ask-E 基准上的校准率低于50%，留有较大改进空间。在 Ask-E 环境上进行训练能够提升多个下游数学基准的表现，且该提升不依赖新增数学数据、不与更强模型交互、不使用基于正确性的奖励信号。

Conclusion: Ask-E 提供了一种新的范式：通过问题生成而非问题回答来评估和提升模型能力。该框架揭示了当前前沿模型在问题校准能力上的显著不足，并展示了一种无需额外监督即可提升下游任务表现的有效训练途径。

Abstract: Today, we improve models by training and evaluating them on problems at the frontier of their abilities. Creating such problems is itself a demanding task, requiring the ability to probe model limits and generalize beyond existing question distributions. It also means placing problems at a precise difficulty level, which requires understanding what it takes to solve them. In short, generating problems calibrated to a model's current frontier demands capability beyond it, an increasingly burdensome constraint as models improve. Our key insight is that we can leverage this constraint to our advantage: a model that can generate problems consistently calibrated to a given frontier must possess capability beyond it. Accordingly, we present Ask-E, an environment that benchmarks and trains models on their ability to write questions at a given skill level, rather than answer them. Concretely, we define target skill levels as ranges bounded by the capabilities of two existing language models. A generated question is successfully calibrated if exactly one of the two models can solve it, placing it precisely within the target range and differentiating the capabilities of these models. Ask-E serves both as a benchmark and a training environment, where models generate problems calibrated to a variety of skill levels. We find that even frontier models achieve below 50% calibration on the benchmark, leaving significant headroom to measure future progress. We also show that training on this environment leads to improvements across a number of downstream math benchmarks even with no new math data, no interaction with stronger models, and no correctness-based reward.

</details>


### [103] [Explicit, Not Longer: What Makes Epistemic Stance Survive Memory Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06953)
*Alex Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体记忆系统中，声言的 epistemic standing（认知立场）如何在压缩存储过程中得以保留。通过在两种模型上开展对照实验，发现将立场以标注字段（labelled field）而非括号旁注（bracketed aside）形式写入，可将保留率提升约15个百分点。预注册复现进一步验证了这一效应。消融实验表明，关键在于使立场显式化（explicit），而非单纯增加长度，且最佳显式化方式因模型而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体记忆系统在存储时会对内容进行压缩，而压缩天然倾向于丢弃修饰性限定语（qualifiers），导致声言的 epistemic standing（如确定性、推测性等认知立场）在写入记忆后往往无法保留。当前缺乏对何种因素决定立场能否在压缩中幸存的理解，这直接关系到记忆系统所存储信息的可靠性与可溯源性。

Method: 研究设计了匹配笔记（matched notes）实验范式：笔记承载完全相同的声言和立场，仅立场的位置形式不同（标注字段 vs. 括号旁注）。两种模型在相同预算下对笔记进行压缩，盲法评估者（不接触实验条件）对结果评分。实验覆盖7个语域中的60条声言。进一步开展消融实验，分离格式（format）、长度（length）和完整句子措辞等成分的贡献。在Haiku模型上进行了预注册复现（prediction和decision rule提前锁定）。还使用了确定性读出器（无模型）进行对照，并获取了50个人工标签（kappa=0.75）用于一致性验证。同时报告了9条被撤回的声言。

Result: 主要结果：立场以标注字段形式写入（相较于括号旁注），在两种模型上分别将保留率从37/60提升至2/60，以及从30/60提升至8/60（permutation p=0.00005）。预注册复现显示Haiku上提升15.6个百分点（38→1）。消融实验揭示：标签（labels）在两种模型上均有益（+9.7和+12.8）；长度在两种模型上均无效；完整句子措辞在一种模型上贡献最大（+12.5），在另一种上几乎无效（+0.6）。确定性读出器可复现方向效应及7个消融对比中的5个，但无法复现长度和标签效应。50个人工标注在方向上一致，kappa=0.75。报告了9条被撤回的声言，其中3条曾为本文的标题声言。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：使立场显式化（而非仅增加长度）是保留 epistemic standing 的关键，且最佳显式化方式依赖于具体模型。单一模型的分析会指向不同且自信的机制解释，因此只能可靠地声言两者的交集。确定性读出器的结果提示，长度和标签效应不应在单一仪器上进行推广。该研究对记忆增强智能体系统设计中立场信息的格式化策略具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Agent memory systems compress what they store, and compression is built to drop qualifiers, so a claim's epistemic standing tends not to survive being written to memory. We ask what governs whether it does. Matched notes carry the identical claim and identical stance and differ only in where that stance sits; one model compresses both under the same budget among the same filler notes, and a blind reader that never sees the condition scores the result. Across 60 claims in seven registers, writing the stance as a labelled field rather than a bracketed aside raises retention by about 15 points on two models (37 claims to 2 on one, 30 to 8 on the other; permutation p=0.00005), and a pre-registered replication on Haiku, its prediction and decision rule committed before the run, gives +15.6 points, 38 claims to 1. Ablating the format on both models gives the same net effect from different parts: labels help on both (+9.7 and +12.8) and length helps on neither, but wording the stance as a full sentence is the largest component on one model (+12.5) and worth nothing on the other (+0.6). Either model alone would have licensed a confident and different mechanism, so we claim only the intersection: make the stance explicit, not merely longer, and expect the best way of being explicit to depend on the model. A deterministic readout with no model reproduces the two-cell direction and five of seven ablation contrasts, but not length or labels, which we therefore do not claim on one instrument. Fifty hand labels (kappa=0.75) agree on direction; we print their seven disagreements in full. We also report nine withdrawn claims, three of them former title claims of this paper.

</details>


### [104] [Can Language Models Imagine Without Seeing? Ekphrasis: Measuring Visual Creative Ideation in Text-Only LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06967)
*Hongyu Luo,He Wang,Huihao Jing,Hong Ting Tsang,Yuxuan Liu,Wuganjing Song,Yauwai Yim,Chunyang Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出视觉创意构思（VCI）基准Ekphrasis，用于评估纯文本语言模型在图像生成前生成原创视觉概念的能力，包含400个任务，覆盖抽象、组合、变换和改编四种类型，通过配对比较和Bradley-Terry模型量化有用性、表现力和新颖性三个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估无法将纯文本语言模型的视觉概念原创能力与流畅文笔分离开来——流畅的文字可能掩盖视觉规划的失败，模型可能只是重复视觉陈词滥调而未能给出可渲染的场景。需要一种独立于文笔质量、专门衡量视觉构思能力的评估方法。

Method: 定义VCI（视觉创意构思）的三个维度：有用性（usefulness）、表现力（expressiveness）和群体新颖性（population-novelty）。构建Ekphrasis基准，含400个任务，覆盖抽象、组合、变换和改编四类。采用匿名配对比较，配合维度特定检查表评分，用Bradley-Terry模型聚合偏好，并引入Typed Idea Graphs将任务特定的群体陈词滥调转化为新颖性参考。还进行了跨模态 grounding 研究，验证文本层面的VCI排序在图像渲染和盲评中的一致性。

Result: 在14个语言模型上的实验表明：VCI能将有用性、表现力和新颖性三个维度有效分离，而非退化为单一的流畅性指标；强模型可通过不同的能力剖面达到相近的总体得分；有用的视觉规划仍可能停留在陈词滥调层面。跨模态研究进一步证实，文本层面的VCI排序在经过忠实渲染和盲法图像偏好判断后仍基本保持不变。

Conclusion: Ekphrasis能够作为超越文笔质量的视觉构思能力有效度量，成功将VCI分解为可区分的有用性、表现力和新颖性维度，为评估语言模型的视觉创意能力提供了可靠的基准工具。

Abstract: Current evaluations do not isolate whether text-only language models can originate visual concepts before image generation. Fluent visual prose can hide visual-plan failures: an answer may appear creative while repeating familiar visual clichés or failing to specify a renderable scene. We define Visual Creative Ideation (VCI) as the ability to produce textual visual plans that are useful, expressive, and population-novel, and introduce Ekphrasis, a 400-task benchmark spanning Abstraction, Combination, Transformation, and Adaptation. Ekphrasis scores anonymized pairwise comparisons with dimension-specific checklists, aggregates preferences with Bradley-Terry models, and uses Typed Idea Graphs to convert task-specific population clichés into novelty references. Across 14 language models, VCI separates usefulness, expressiveness, and novelty rather than reducing to fluency: strong models achieve similar overall scores through different profiles, and useful plans can remain visually clichéd. A cross-modal grounding study further shows that text-level VCI ordering largely survives faithful rendering and blind image-level preference judgment, supporting Ekphrasis as a measure of visual ideation beyond prose quality.

</details>


### [105] [PHASE-Tree: Modeling Character-State Evolution in Long-Horizon Role-Playing Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06975)
*Bo Tang,Jianan Yang,Junyi Zhu,Yiquan Wu,Rui Zhao,Zhengyu Yang,Yang Zhang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li,Jiajun Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PHASE-Tree 提出多层时间尺度角色状态树，支持局部化、可寻址的角色状态更新，结合 LongEvoRoleBench 基准，在长篇对话角色扮演中实现了显著的进化状态生成性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程角色扮演存在两个核心缺陷：(1) 角色表征是静态的，无法在不影响未改变特质的情况下进行局部更新；(2) 现有基准仅评估角色一致性和记忆召回，未衡量模型是否能从角色当前进化状态出发进行生成。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出 PHASE-Tree（多时间尺度角色状态树），包含不可变的身份根节点和可变的人格、会话、时刻三层，使每个可变字段成为可寻址的目标，支持局部的集内和跨集更新。生成条件可通过显式文本注入或隐式参数自适应实现。同时构建 LongEvoRoleBench 基准，将四个长对话语料（跨集进化）与四个短对话语料（场景内状态追踪检查）配对，统一采用下一句预测协议。

Result: 在长对话核心测试中，文本型 PHASE-Tree 在 12 个数据集-指标组合中对内部变体赢下 11 个、对外部文本基线赢下全部 12 个，角色级、语义和嵌入分数分别提升 19.7%、12.4% 和 15.1%。盲法 200 响应人工评估中，人类评分与 GPT-4.1 评判的 Pearson r=0.65；在描述性 n=10 的 PT 和 NR 提示子集上，总体差异为 +0.20。长对话语义优势在不同 LLM 评判者和生成骨干上均保持稳健。

Conclusion: PHASE-Tree 通过层级化、可局部更新的角色状态树有效解决了长程角色扮演中静态表征与进化状态生成评估缺失的问题，在多项自动和人工评估中均表现出显著优势，且跨评判者和模型骨干具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Long-horizon role-playing demands that characters remain recognizable as they evolve with the narrative. Yet existing work falls short on two fronts: representations are typically static profiles that cannot be updated locally without destabilizing unchanged traits, and benchmarks mainly test persona preservation and memory recall rather than whether a model speaks from a character's currently evolved state. We address both. PHASE-Tree is a multi-timescale character-state tree with an immutable identity root and mutable persona, session, and moment layers, making each mutable field an addressable target for localized within- and cross-episode updates. It conditions generation through explicit textual provision or implicit parametric adaptation. To measure evolved-state generation, we introduce LongEvoRoleBench, which pairs four long-dialogue corpora for cross-episode evolution with four short-dialogue corpora as within-scene state-tracking checks, under a unified next-utterance protocol. On the long-dialogue core, textual PHASE-Tree ranks first in 11 of 12 dataset-metric cells against internal variants and all 12 cells against external textual baselines, improving character-level, semantic, and embedding scores by 19.7%, 12.4%, and 15.1% respectively. In a blinded 200-response study, human ratings correlate with the GPT-4.1 judge (Pearson r= 0.65); on descriptive n= 10 PT and NR prompt subsets, the Overall difference is +0.20. The long-dialogue Sem advantage persists across LLM judges and generation backbones.

</details>


### [106] [Confirming Our Biases? Evaluating the Capabilities, Risks, and Societal Impact of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06977)
*Mudar Adas,Polina Tsvilodub,Michael Franke,Martin V. Butz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了六种大语言模型在面对160个涵盖观点类和事实类主题的提示时，如何系统性地使回答与提示框架对齐。结果表明，即使在事实性领域，提示框架也能压倒事实一致性，LLM会强化用户偏见且易受显性操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究确认LLM对提示框架敏感，但尚不清楚LLM在多大程度上会强化用户偏见，以及隐性框架效应与显性提示操纵之间的边界在哪里。本研究旨在系统评估LLM在直接性和暗示性提示下，支持或挑战特定立场的易感性。

Method: 评估六种LLM，使用160个不同提示，覆盖十个主题（横跨观点类和事实类领域）。提示在策略（支持vs.挑战指令）、极性、用户表达信念及主题领域等维度上系统变化，全面考察LLM的回答对齐行为。

Result: LLM会系统性地调整回答以对齐提示框架，即使在事实性语境中也是如此。提示框架可以压倒事实一致性，模型在理应保持事实稳定的领域同样表现出被操纵的倾向。

Conclusion: 研究界定了LLM可操纵性的程度与边界。LLM不仅会强化用户微妙的偏见，而且在要求事实稳定的领域也易受显性提示操纵，这对LLM的安全部署具有重要启示。

Abstract: It is well established that large language models (LLMs) are sensitive to prompt framing, reflecting patterns in their training data or prior prompts. In this study, we investigate the extent to which LLMs reinforce users biases expressed in the prompts and examine the boundary between implicit framing effects and explicit prompt manipulation. Specifically, we evaluate how susceptible LLMs are to direct and suggestive prompts that encourage models to support or challenge particular positions.
  We evaluate six LLMs using 160 distinct prompts spanning ten topics across opinion-based and factual domains. The prompts systematically vary in prompting strategy, support versus challenge instructions, prompt polarity, users' expressed beliefs, and topic domain, spanning both opinion-based and factual questions. Our results show that LLMs systematically adapt their responses to align with prompt framing, even in factual contexts. This suggests that prompt framing can outweigh factual consistency in model responses. Overall, our findings delineate the extent and boundaries of LLM manipulability. Furthermore, the results imply that LLMs can reinforce subtle user biases and are susceptible to explicit prompt manipulation even in domains where responses should remain factually stable.

</details>


### [107] [GPTKB 2.0: Browsing, Querying, and Auditing a Disambiguated LLM-Derived Knowledge Base](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06992)
*Yujia Hu,Tuan-Phong Nguyen,Simon Razniewski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GPTKB 2.0 是一个从大语言模型自动构建的大规模消歧知识库，包含3840万三元组、160万规范实体，并提供可交互的Web演示，支持浏览、溯源和SPARQL查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM派生的知识库大多仅通过表面字符串来标识实体，无法解决同名异义词和同义词合并问题。GPTKB 2.0旨在通过上下文引导的消歧机制，在递归构建过程中分离同音异义词并合并同义指称，同时提供透明的溯源审计能力。

Method: 采用递归式知识库构建流程，在事实抽取过程中引入上下文引导的实体消歧：对每个实体提及，生成候选匹配并基于上下文做消歧决策，分离同音异义词（homonyms）、合并同义指称（synonymous mentions）。同时记录完整的溯源信息，包括表面形式、候选匹配、源三元组及消歧决策。演示界面支持实体浏览、跨链接导航、事实溯源审计、SPARQL结构化查询、自然语言转SPARQL以及实体链接。

Result: GPTKB 2.0 最终包含3840万三元组，覆盖160万规范实体、20.76万合并关系和6.6万合并类别。Web演示系统使整个消歧和构建过程可检验，支持用户通过多种方式探索和审计知识库。

Conclusion: GPTKB 2.0 展示了LLM驱动的知识库构建中引入实体消歧的可行性与价值，其透明、可审计的设计为大规模自动知识图谱构建提供了可信的演示平台，知识库已开放在线访问和离线下载。

Abstract: We present a web demo for exploring a large-scale disambiguated knowledge base (KB) materialized from a large language model (LLM). GPTKB 2.0 contains 38.4M triples over 1.6M canonical entities, together with 207.6K consolidated relations and 66K consolidated classes. Unlike prior LLM-derived knowledge bases that largely identify entities by surface strings, GPTKB 2.0 performs context-guided disambiguation during recursive KB construction, separating homonyms and merging synonymous mentions as facts are elicited. The demo makes this process inspectable: users can browse entities, follow links across the KB, and audit the provenance of individual facts, including surface forms, candidate matches, source triples, and disambiguation decisions. The interface further supports structured SPARQL queries, natural-language questions translated to SPARQL, and entity linking from user-provided text to canonical GPTKB 2.0 entries. GPTKB 2.0 is available at https://gptkb.org/, with the full KB downloadable for offline use.

</details>


### [108] [Does More Retrieved Evidence Help Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Diffusion Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07006)
*Jiankun Wang,Yisen Gao,Ziwei Zhang,Xingcheng Fu,Jiaxin Bai,Chen Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散语言模型(DLMs)在视觉RAG中面临"检索越多反而越差"的反直觉现象，根源在于并行去噪过程中的语义冲突，并提出无需训练的熵基候选过滤框架(ECF)来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG假设"检索证据越多越好"，但作者发现这一假设对扩散语言模型不成立：更多检索页虽提升答案覆盖率，但无条件传入生成器反而降低准确率。核心原因是并行去噪中的语义冲突——不同来源的视觉证据在位置级去噪中相互干扰，产生不可信答案。亟需一种证据筛选机制，在保持召回率的同时限制有害视觉暴露。

Method: 提出ECF (Entropy-Based Candidate Filter)，一种无需训练的证据准入框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 构建多粒度证据单元以减少单条候选内的无关内容；(2) 利用空白控制块置信度(blank-controlled block confidence)与检索排序，判定是否及哪些候选应进入最终上下文。该方法利用第一步答案块分布的熵信息，在解码前评估证据质量。

Result: 在3个多模态DLM和5个视觉QA基准上，ECF相比最强固定top-k输入平均提升2.62个百分点；在LLaDA2.0-Uni上，相比每个数据集的最佳免训练竞争方法平均提升2.37个百分点。

Conclusion: 扩大检索确实有利于视觉DLM-RAG，但关键在于选择性证据准入(selective evidence admission)而非无条件证据扩展(unconditional evidence expansion)。ECF为扩散语言模型在检索增强场景中的证据管理提供了有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) commonly expands the retrieved evidence set to improve answer-page coverage, implicitly assuming that all available evidence should be passed to the generator. We show that this assumption does not hold for diffusion language models (DLMs): retrieving more pages increases answer-page recall, whereas unconditionally passing all retrieved pages to the generator often reduces answer accuracy, primarily because of semantic conflict. A latent-source analysis explains this mismatch through source-coherence loss in parallel denoising, where position-wise proposals can combine incompatible visual sources into unsupported answers. We further find that such interference is already visible in the first-step answer-block distribution, making it possible to assess evidence before decoding. To preserve retrieval coverage while limiting harmful visual exposure, we propose the Entropy-Based Candidate Filter (ECF), a training-free evidence-admission framework. To reduce irrelevant content within individual candidates, ECF constructs multi-granularity evidence units; to identify beneficial additional evidence, it uses blank-controlled block confidence and retrieval rank to determine whether and which candidate should enter the final context. Across three multimodal DLMs and five visual QA benchmarks, ECF improves answer accuracy by 2.62 percentage points on average over the strongest fixed top-$k$ input and, with LLaDA2.0-Uni, by 2.37 percentage points on average over the best competing training-free result for each dataset. These results show that broader retrieval benefits visual DLM-RAG through selective evidence admission rather than unconditional evidence expansion. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/wjkuser/ECF.

</details>


### [109] [An Agentic Hybrid Top-Down and Bottom-Up Approach to Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07023)
*Emma Jouffroy,Warren Jouanneau,Marc Palyart*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合知识图谱生成流程，将大语言模型与Wikidata多语言知识图谱相结合，通过智能体反射模式处理多语言技能声明中的标准化、去重和新概念发现，最终从非结构化文本中构建可扩展、可解释的技能知识图谱和分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HR平台面临海量非标准化、多语言的专业技能声明难以有效组织的核心挑战，这直接影响人才匹配等下游任务的准确性。现有方法要么是僵化的自上而下方法，要么是碎片化的自下而上方法，均难以应对快速演化、噪声严重的多语言技能数据。

Method: 提出五阶段混合知识图谱生成流程：(1) 实体对齐——将识别到的概念锚定到Wikidata稳定实体；(2) 多语言规范化——跨五种欧洲语言进行概念标准化；(3) 主动策展——人工介入的关键审核环节；(4) 去重——消除冗余实体；(5) 未映射概念的迭代恢复——采用智能体反射模式（agentic reflexion pattern）动态创建新节点和关系元数据，合成新兴概念。整个系统以大语言模型为核心引擎，Wikidata多语言KG为锚定基础。

Result: 系统能够自主适应五种欧洲语言中快速演化且充满噪声的技能提及，实现高度可扩展、可解释且具备自修复能力的技能知识图谱生成，并从中推导出结构化的技能分类体系。

Conclusion: 该混合流程为从非结构化噪声文本中构建全面的技能知识图谱提供了可行的解决方案，通过结合稳定知识图谱实体与动态新概念创建，在可扩展性、可解释性和自适应性之间取得了良好平衡，对HR领域的人才匹配等下游任务具有重要意义。

Abstract: Organizing thousands of unstandardized, multilingual expertise declarations is a persistent challenge for Human Resources (HR) platforms, directly impacting downstream tasks like accurate talent matching. To address this, we propose a hybrid knowledge graph generation pipeline that grounds a Large Language Model (LLM) in the Wikidata multilingual Knowledge Graph (KG) while employing an agentic reflexion pattern to synthesize emerging concepts and their associated metadata. Unlike rigid top-down methods or fragmented bottom-up approaches, our system anchors recognized concepts to stable Knowledge Graph entities while dynamically creating new nodes and relational metadata for unrecognized skills. Executed across five stages, entity reconciliation, multilingual canonicalization, active curation, deduplication, and the iterative recovery of unmapped concepts, the system autonomously adapts to rapidly evolving, noisy skill mentions across five European languages. Ultimately, this pipeline provides a highly scalable, explicable, and self-healing framework for generating a comprehensive skills knowledge graph, from which a structured taxonomy is derived, using unstructured, noisy text.

</details>


### [110] [HNR-DAC: Hard-Negative Reranking and Distribution-Aligned Classification for Scientific Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07204)
*Zhenchao Wang,Xin Chen,Luoxi Zhang,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HNR-DAC 是一个两阶段框架，通过硬负例重排序（HNR）量化证据混淆度并对比训练，以及分布对齐分类（DAC）在推断时实际使用的段落上训练分类器，在科学声明验证任务中取得了优异表现，在 NLPCC 2026 官方榜单排名第三，且 Macro-F1 最高达 93.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学声明验证面临两个核心挑战：（1）论文内部的干扰段落常常与真实证据高度相似，容易误导模型；（2）分类器在训练时使用黄金证据，但推断时却必须基于检索到的证据运行，造成训练-推断分布不一致。现有方法未充分解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出 HNR-DAC 两阶段框架。第一阶段 Hard-Negative Reranking（HNR）：利用基础重排序器对非黄金段落的打分来量化证据混淆度，将黄金证据与最易混淆的候选段落进行对比训练。第二阶段 Distribution-Aligned Classification（DAC）：使用冻结的 HNR 产生的 Top-1 段落来训练分类器，实现训练与推断输入的分布对齐；同时 HNR 的 Top-3 段落标识符直接作为证据输出。

Result: 最终配置在 NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2 上获得 97.21% Hit@3、95.79% Macro-F1、94.47% Joint@3，平均分 95.13%。官方提交排名第三，Macro-F1 达 93.05%（全场最高），Joint@3 为 70.16%，平均分 81.61%。

Conclusion: HNR-DAC 有效解决了科学声明验证中的证据混淆和分布偏移问题，通过让每个阶段都在实际部署条件下训练，显著提升了证据识别和关系分类的性能，尤其在 Macro-F1 指标上表现突出。

Abstract: Scientific claim verification over a cited paper requires predicting the claim--paper relation and identifying the paragraphs that justify that prediction. This setting poses two linked challenges: within-paper distractors often resemble genuine evidence, while a classifier trained on gold evidence must operate on retrieved evidence at inference. We present HNR-DAC, a two-stage framework that trains each stage on the cases it will actually encounter. Hard-Negative Reranking (HNR) quantifies evidence confusability using a base reranker's scores on non-gold paragraphs and contrasts gold evidence against the most confusable candidates. Distribution-Aligned Classification (DAC) trains on the Top-1 paragraph produced by the same frozen HNR used to construct inference inputs, while HNR's Top-3 paragraph identifiers provide the evidence output. On the NLPCC 2026 Task 10 Track 2, the final configuration obtains 97.21% Hit@3, 95.79% Macro-F1, 94.47% Joint@3, and an average score of 95.13%. The corresponding submission ranks third on the official Track 2 leaderboard while achieving the highest overall Macro-F1 of 93.05%, alongside 70.16% Joint@3 and an average score of 81.61%.

</details>


### [111] [Measuring Concept Content in Text from LLM Activations: ESG Evidence from Concept Vectors and Linear Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07208)
*Luc Hazenoot,Zhaochun Ren,Amirhossein Zohrehvand*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用冻结的大语言模型内部激活值来测量文本的概念内容，而非依赖传统表面特征或模型显式回答。通过在ESG金融文本数据集上实验，线性探针方法取得了接近微调领域分类器的准确率，且持续优于模型自身的问答表现，表明LLM内部知识蕴含了其回复中未报告的概念信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本概念测量方法仅关注文本表面（词频、主题比例、嵌入相似度），忽视了读者形成的判断。同时，LLM内部知识与外在表达之间存在差距。本文旨在探究：冻结的、开箱即用的LLM内部激活值能否替代任务特定微调来测量概念内容，以及哪种提取方法效果最佳。

Method: 作者采用递归特征机（RFM）算法和线性探针（linear probing）从冻结LLM的激活值中提取概念度量，并与嵌入基线、表面基线（词典词份额等）以及模型自身对问题的回答进行对比。实验基于人工标注的ESG（环境、社会与治理）金融文本数据集。

Result: 最佳线性探针的准确率与微调领域分类器仅差0.6个百分点，且无需任何任务特定微调。在12组对比中，线性探针在11组上优于模型自身的问答表现，证明激活值中蕴含了模型回复未报告的概念内容。简单探针持续优于RFM概念向量，但RFM提供了分类所不具备的连续评分，可反映概念在文本中的强弱程度。

Conclusion: 冻结LLM的内部激活值可以作为概念内容测量的有效代理，接近甚至媲美微调模型的性能。线性探针是更优的提取方法，而RFM提供的连续评分在缺乏分级标签时有独特价值。

Abstract: Existing measures of how much a text is about a concept read the surface of the text: dictionary word shares, topic proportions, embedding similarities. They score the words a text uses, not the judgment a reader forms about it. Recent work has shown that a gap exists in what Large Language Models (LLMs) know internally versus what they express in their response. This paper asks whether that internal knowledge, read by monitoring the activations of frozen, out-of-the-box LLMs, can stand in for task-specific fine-tuning when measuring concept content, and which extraction method reads it best. We extract such measures via the Recursive Feature Machine (RFM) algorithm and via linear probing, and compare these against an embedding baseline, surface baselines, and the same model's own answer to the question. We demonstrate the approach on financial text, a domain studied extensively and served by established annotated resources, using a human-annotated Environmental, Social and Governance (ESG) dataset. The best linear probe comes within 0.6 percentage points of a fine-tuned domain classifier's accuracy without any task-specific fine-tuning, and outscores the same model's own answer to the question in eleven of twelve comparisons, so the activations carry concept content the response does not report. The simple probe consistently beats the RFM concept vectors, which in turn provide what classification alone does not: a continuous score intended to reflect how strongly a concept is present in a text, whose validation awaits graded labels.

</details>


### [112] [From Test-Time Scaling to Reusable Memory: Measuring Crystallization in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07213)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yuanxi Che,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"结晶问题"（crystallization problem）框架，用于衡量文本到SQL系统中存储已修正查询的长期价值。通过在BIRD基准上的控制实验，发现存储已验证的正确查询可使未见问题的首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，占按需修复所能提供精度空间的44.4%，并识别出数据库特定内容是记忆增益的核心因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SQL系统在测试时通过额外计算修复错误查询，但修复经验通常被丢弃；即便系统开始保留已验证的修复记录，评估仍仅报告端到端分数，无法区分记忆对重复问题的回放收益与对新问题的迁移收益，也无法识别哪种记忆策略真正起作用。作者将此度量缺口定义为"结晶问题"。

Method: 采用控制实验范式：固定单次求解器不变，每次仅改变一个记忆变量。在BIRD基准上分别测量：(1)回放（replay）——对相同问题的增益；(2)跨问题保留（cross-question retention）；(3)留出同数据库迁移（held-out same-database transfer）。通过受控干预逐项识别各记忆成分的贡献。

Result: 存储已验证的正确查询使留出测试集首次尝试准确率提升4.34个百分点，这一增益捕获了按需修复在同问题上所能提供精度上限的44.4%。受控干预实验表明，数据库特定内容是主要有效成分；可靠的验证机制和更广泛的检索覆盖带来持续增益，而更丰富的存储格式和复杂检索器则未产生额外收益。

Conclusion: 系统性地积累已验证的SQL修复经验对提升未见问题的泛化性能具有实质性价值。"结晶问题"框架为评估记忆机制的未来价值提供了方法论基础。数据库特定内容（而非格式或检索器的复杂性）是驱动增益的关键，这为构建高效的文本到SQL记忆系统指明了方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can correct difficult text-to-SQL queries, but the extra computation is normally discarded after each answer. Systems increasingly retain verified repair episodes, yet evaluations still report one end-to-end score. It cannot distinguish replay on recurring questions from help on unseen questions, or identify the responsible memory choice. We call measuring this future value the crystallization problem. Our controlled evaluation holds the single-shot solver fixed and varies one memory choice at a time. We separately measure replay, cross-question retention, and held-out same-database transfer. On BIRD, storing verified corrected queries improves held-out first-attempt accuracy by 4.34 percentage points. This gain captures 44.4% of the accuracy headroom provided by on-demand repair on the same questions. Controlled interventions identify database-specific content as the main operating ingredient. Reliable verification and broader retrieval coverage yield supported gains; richer formats and elaborate retrievers do not. Open-source code, evaluation artifacts, and reproduction instructions are available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/text-to-sql-memory-crystallization.

</details>


### [113] [Skaling: Chinchilla's Exponents Meet Kaplan's Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07222)
*Mathurin Videau,Badr Youbi-Idrissi,David Lopez-Paz,Kartik Ahuja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Skaling定律通过引入模型容量与训练数据之间的单一交互指数，将两者耦合起来，解决了标准神经规模定律在数据稀缺和过度训练极端情况下系统性低估或高估损失的问题。该方法可将平均绝对百分比误差降低1.5-3倍，并以约10倍更少的计算量实现精确的全网格外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经规模定律（如Kaplan等人的公式）假设模型大小和训练数据对损失的影响是相互独立的，这一假设在数据稀缺（undertraining）和过度训练（overtraining）的极端区域导致损失预测出现系统性偏差。作者旨在提出一个更通用的函数形式来修正这一根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出Skaling定律，是一种将模型容量与训练数据通过单一交互指数耦合的广义函数形式。具体包括：1）在损失函数中引入模型大小与数据量之间的交互项；2）采用稀疏网格策略，仅在低计算量区域进行实验采样，即可外推至全网格。

Result: 1）Skaling定律在插值和外推场景下，将平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）降低了1.5-3倍；2）结合稀疏网格策略，仅需均匀扫描约1/10的计算量即可实现精确的全网格外推。

Conclusion: Skaling定律通过引入模型-数据的耦合交互项，为下一代大模型训练中的计算预算分配提供了更鲁棒、更资源高效的预测框架，使得从小规模实验可靠预测大规模性能成为可能。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws are foundational for language model development, yet standard formulations systematically under- and overestimate loss at data-scarce and overtraining extremes. This failure originates in the underlying assumption that model size and training data impact the loss independently. To address this, we introduce the Skaling law, a generalized functional form that couples model capacity and data through a single interaction exponent. This simple extension reduces the Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) by 1.5-3x across both interpolation and extrapolation regimes. When paired with a sparse grid strategy restricted to low-compute regimes, the Skaling law achieves accurate full-grid extrapolation using approximately 10x less compute than uniform sweeps. By enabling reliable performance prediction from small-scale experiments, the Skaling law provides a more robust and resource-efficient framework for allocating compute budgets in next-generation model training.

</details>


### [114] [Stoicheia: Character-Level Masked Diffusion for Ancient Greek Textual Restoration, Parsing, and Metrical Scansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07249)
*Eric Cullhed,Albin Thörn Cleland*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Stoicheia是一个405M参数的字符级掩码扩散编码器，针对古希腊语设计。它能在统一骨干上同时处理字母、词/句边界、变音符、大小写和标点五个对齐平面，从而无需任务特定重分词即可完成文本补全、分词、标音、标点等任务。在碑文修复、句法解析和长音标注三项任务上均显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古希腊语文本面临的典型挑战是多维度的：缺乏词间空格、变音符可能缺失、标点不完整、碑铭有残缺。现有方法通常将这些子任务独立处理，需要任务特定的重分词。作者提出用一个统一的字符级模型同时建模文本的多个正交维度，实现"一次预训练，多重下游能力"。

Method: 提出Stoicheia，一个405M参数的字符级掩码扩散编码器。其核心创新是将输入分解为五个对齐且可独立掩码的平面：字母、词和句边界、变音符、大小写、标点。采用掩码扩散预训练，在3.8亿词的开放、版本固定的古希腊语语料上进行。发布11个检查点：10个旋转、去污染的fold（保证任一文段至少有一个模型未见过），1个未接触文献文本。下游评估采用匹配随机初始化对照组，严格隔离预训练贡献。

Result: 在碑文/纸莎草修复上降低5.6 CER点；依存句法解析提升12.9 LAS；长音标注提升6.0点平衡准确率。在Ithaca测试集上，相同冻结样本和严格评分下，字符错误率从Ithaca的24.6和Aeneas的23.5降至15.5，Top-1准确率从63.0/64.0提升至74.5。

Conclusion: 字符级多平面掩码扩散预训练为古希腊语文本处理提供了一种统一且高效的范式，在多项核心任务上实现对现有专用系统的显著超越，证明了统一建模文本多维正交属性的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce Stoicheia, a 405M-parameter character-level masked-diffusion encoder for Ancient Greek whose input factors into five aligned, independently maskable planes: letters, word and sentence boundaries, diacritics, capitalization, and punctuation. A single backbone can therefore restore lacunae, re-segment, accentuate, and punctuate unspaced text without task-specific retokenization. We pretrain it on an open, revision-pinned corpus of 380M words and release eleven checkpoints: ten rotated, decontaminated folds, guaranteeing that for any given literary passage at least one released model has never seen its text, and one with no exposure to documentary texts. Three experiments - reconstruction of damaged inscriptions and papyri, morphosyntactic tagging and dependency parsing, and macronization with metrical scansion - each carry a matched random-initialization control, isolating what character-level diffusion pretraining contributes: 5.6 CER points on inscription reconstruction, 12.9 LAS on parsing, and 6.0 points of balanced accuracy on macronization. On Ithaca's own test split, with identical frozen samples and strict scoring, Stoicheia reduces character error relative to both prior state-of-the-art systems, from 24.6 (Ithaca) and 23.5 (its 2025 Aeneas-framework successor) to 15.5, and raises top-1 accuracy from 63.0 and 64.0 to 74.5.

</details>


### [115] [Why Knowing Both Hops Is Not Enough: Understanding Two-Hop Generalization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07261)
*Zili Zhang,Yilin Wang,Heng Wang,Herun Wan,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控符号环境下的机制分析，揭示了两跳推理中泛化成功与失败的根本原因：成功源于跨上下文的中间表征一致性，失败源于上下层功能不匹配。基于此提出循环式训练策略，显著改善了分布外两跳查询的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型能解决复杂多跳问题，却在简单的两跳查询上表现出令人困惑的失败：模型可以正确存储每个单独的跳，但常常无法将它们组合起来。本文旨在理解这一现象的内部机制。

Method: 在受控符号环境中从头训练Transformer，通过机制可解释性分析（mechanistic analysis）研究两跳泛化行为。具体分析了中间表征在不同上下文中的一致性，以及上下层网络的功能分工。在此基础上，提出了循环式训练策略（recurrent-style training strategy），使模型能够跨输入形式复用推理回路。

Result: 发现了一个清晰的泛化模式：当第二跳遵循训练分布时模型可靠泛化，当第二跳偏离训练分布时模型总是失败。机制分析表明，成功泛化由跨上下文中相同实体的一致中间表征驱动；而失败源于层间不匹配——下层正确构建了中间表征，但上层虽然在对应原子事实上训练过，却主要学习将其映射到输出而非对其进行推理。循环式训练策略显著改善了分布外两跳查询的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 两跳推理泛化失败并非因为模型缺乏知识，而是推理回路在层间出现了功能断裂：下层编码、上层解码，缺乏中间推理环节。通过循环式训练强制模型复用推理回路，可有效弥合这一鸿沟，为改进LLM的多跳推理能力提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve complex multi-hop problems yet exhibit puzzling failures on simple two-hop queries: although a model may correctly store each individual hop, it often fails to combine them. To understand the internal mechanisms of this phenomenon, we train transformers from scratch in a controlled symbolic environment. Our experiments reveal a pattern in two-hop generalization: models generalize reliably when the second hop follows the training distribution, but always fail when it deviates.
  Through mechanistic analysis, we provide a complete explanation for these distinct generalization behaviors: in settings where models generalize successfully, performance is driven by the emergence of consistent intermediate representations for the same entities across contexts, whereas failures on settings where the second hop is out-of-distribution arise from a mismatch across layers: lower layers correctly construct these intermediate representations, but upper layers, while trained on corresponding atomic facts, primarily learn to map them to outputs rather than to reason over them.
  Driven by this insight, we propose a recurrent-style training strategy, which enables transformers to reuse their reasoning circuitry across input forms and substantially improves generalization on out-of-distribution two-hop queries.

</details>


### [116] [Gaze Behavior in Visual World Experiments Can be Modeled With Off-the-shelf Language-Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07282)
*Rahul Murali Shankar,Titus von der Malsburg,Sebastian Padó*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合CLIP多模态双编码器与双模态归因方法的新方案，用于预测视觉世界范式中的眼动注视行为，无需生成式架构或微调即可稳健复现人类预测性加工的实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算心理语言学研究几乎全部聚焦于单一模态（书面或口语）的神经语言模型，忽视了视觉世界范式等多模态实验范式。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索神经语言模型能否同样作为人类多模态语言加工的认知模型。

Method: 采用CLIP家族的多模态双编码器模型，结合双模态归因方法（bimodal attribution method），直接预测视觉世界实验中受试者在同时接收视觉与语言输入时的注视行为，无需生成式架构，也无需针对该任务进行微调。

Result: 该方法成功稳健地复现了一项经典英语视觉世界研究的核心发现——人类在语言加工中的预测性加工（predictive processing）效应。

Conclusion: 即使没有生成式架构和任务特定微调，简单的多模态双编码器模型也能有效建模视觉世界范式中的预测性注视行为，表明此类模型有望成为人类多模态语言加工的有力计算模型。

Abstract: The recent advances in neural language models have also spurred much work in computational psycholinguistics, asking whether neural LMs are also promising models of human language processing. However, work has been overwhelmingly focused on the unimodal case of written or spoken language. In contrast, multimodal experimental paradigms, like visual world studies that present participants with both visual and linguistic input simultaneously, have been neglected. In this paper, we present a novel approach that predicts gaze behavior in visual world studies. It does so by combining a simple multi-modal bi-encoder model of the CLIP family with a bimodal attribution method. We demonstrate the ability of this approach to robustly replicate the results of a seminal English visual world study which shows hu- man predictive processing. Remarkably, it does so without a generative architecture and without the need for fine-tuning, despite not being trained for this task.

</details>


### [117] [Grammar Engineering Meets LLMs: Development of Cantonese and Irish ParGram Treebanks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07283)
*Chit-Fung Lam,Elaine Uí Dhonnchadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在ParGram项目框架下构建粤语和爱尔兰语树库的实践，并评估了多语言大语言模型（以OpenAI的gpt-oss-120b为例）在语法工程中辅助翻译和句法结构生成的效果。结果表明LLM在翻译和跨语言抽象任务上表现不佳，但能提供一定参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语法工程，尤其是跨语言平行语法项目，需要平衡跨语言一致性和语言特定属性，对语言学和计算实现的专业知识要求极高。本文旨在探索多语言大语言模型能否以及能在多大程度上辅助这一高成本、高专业门槛的工作流程。

Method: 研究分为两部分：其一，在ParGram项目框架下，以抽象功能层面的语言平行为原则，开发粤语和爱尔兰语树库；其二，使用OpenAI的gpt-oss-120b多语言大模型，分别测试其在粤语-爱尔兰语翻译和形式句法结构生成任务上的表现，并系统分析提示语言等因素对结果的影响。

Result: 翻译方面，模型表现总体不佳，且提示语言的变化对翻译质量无显著影响。句法结构生成方面，模型能产出一部分结构上有意义的输出，但在需要跨语言抽象的任务上表现较差。不过，LLM生成的结果在提供替代分析方案和（部分）捕捉谓词-论元关系方面仍具有参考价值。

Conclusion: LLM在协作式语法工程中存在一定潜力，但目前其局限性更为突出，尤其在跨语言抽象任务上不可靠。专家驱动的分析与验证仍然是语法工程中不可替代的核心环节。

Abstract: Grammar engineering requires expertise in linguistic formalism and computational implementation, especially in parallel grammar projects that balance cross-linguistic consistency with language-specific properties. This paper presents the development of Cantonese and Irish treebanks within the Parallel Grammar (ParGram) Project, where linguistic parallelism is maintained at an abstract functional level. We also investigate the methodological potential and limitations of using multilingual LLMs to support grammar engineering, focusing on Cantonese-Irish translation and the generation of formal syntactic structures using OpenAI's gpt-oss-120b model. The results show that translation performance was generally unsatisfactory and unaffected by prompt language. For syntactic structure generation, the model produced some structurally meaningful outputs, but performed poorly on tasks requiring cross-linguistic abstraction. Nonetheless, LLM-generated outputs may still offer some reference value by suggesting alternative analyses and (partially) capturing predicate-argument relations. Overall, our findings highlight both the potential and limitations of using LLMs in collaborative grammar engineering, while underscoring the continued importance of expert-driven analysis and verification.

</details>


### [118] [Natural Language Processing Psychometrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07316)
*Edoardo Sebastiano De Duro,Emma Franchino,Massimo Stella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"NLP心理测量学"框架，利用9个大语言模型在受控人格设定下完成心理测量问卷，提取情感特征和句法-语义网络结构，结合SHAP可解释AI，预测生活满意度、抑郁、焦虑和压力。结果表明，情感与网络特征在不同心理维度上的预测贡献迥异，且模型能跨任务迁移至真实临床文本分类，但同时揭示了合成数据的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP模型预测心理健康结果时，很少明确其究竟测量的是文本中的语境知识、情感内容还是句法结构，导致可解释性差。作者旨在通过"NLP心理测量学"范式，将基于文本的心理预测视为心理测量问题，将预测分数锚定于可解释的语言学证据上。

Method: 1) 构建9个LLM的"认知数字影子"（受控人格设定），使其完成SWLS、PHQ-9、DASS-21等心理测量问卷并逐项生成文本解释；2) 从文本中提取情感画像和"文本形式心智网络"（forma mentis networks）的句法-语义结构特征；3) 将上述特征结合人格变量和人口学变量，训练随机森林回归器并进行消融实验；4) 使用SHAP值识别驱动预测的关键特征及其影响方向；5) 跨任务验证：将模型应用于真实日记文本和临床对话转录本，检验泛化能力。

Result: 全特征随机森林模型可解释生活满意度70.8%的方差、抑郁(PHQ-9)55.7%的方差，以及DASS-21中抑郁68.5%、焦虑76.0%、压力72.4%的方差。人口学变量对抑郁、焦虑、压力几乎无解释力，但对生活满意度有意义，其中情感特征和收入是最强预测因子；神经质和网络拓扑结构在抑郁和焦虑中占主导，但影响方向相反。跨任务评估中，模型无需重训练即可区分低分与高分日记（r高达0.91），并仅用网络/情感特征在真实临床对话中以最高68%准确率区分临床组与对照组。

Conclusion: 合成数据展现了两面性：LLM人格设定可暴露模型偏差、恢复与临床反刍思维一致的模式，并支持跨格式心理预测，但不能替代人类验证。NLP心理测量学通过可解释AI和网络/情感特征，使这些区分变得明确、可测量和可检验。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) models predicting mental health outcomes rarely specify what they measure: contextual knowledge, emotional content, or syntactic structure. NLP Psychometrics treats psychological prediction from text as a psychometric problem, linking scores to interpretable linguistic evidence and testing beyond the training text format. Nine LLMs, conditioned on controlled personas (cognitive digital shadows), completed psychometric questionnaires with textual explanations per item. We extracted emotional profiles and syntactic-semantic structure via textual forma mentis networks, combined with personality and sociodemographic variables in ablated random forest (RF) regressors, using SHAP to identify which features drove performance and in which direction. Full RF models explained up to 70.8% of variance in life satisfaction (SWLS), 55.7% in depression (PHQ-9), and, for DASS-21, 68.5% depression, 76.0% anxiety, 72.4% stress. Sociodemographics alone explained no meaningful variance in depression, anxiety, or stress, but did so for life satisfaction, where emotion features and income were the strongest predictors; neuroticism and network topology instead dominated depression and anxiety, reversing direction between them. Without retraining, RF models separated diaries from low- and high-score personas ($r$ up to 0.91) and, using only network/emotion features, classified clinical from control participants in real transcripts with up to 68% accuracy. These results show the promise and limits of synthetic data: LLM personas can expose model biases, recover patterns consistent with clinical rumination, and support psychometric prediction from human text without a matched questionnaire, but cannot substitute for human validation. NLP Psychometrics makes these distinctions explicit, measurable, and testable through interpretable AI and network/emotional features.

</details>


### [119] [Zero Gap Is Not Restoration: Stratified Per-Question Probability Evaluation and Step-wise Mitigation of Benchmark Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07341)
*Ruijie Hou,Yueyang Jiao,Zhao Wang,Yingming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有污染缓解评估指标G-AP的缺陷，提出新指标SA-PPG，并设计了RailCap策略，在多个受污染模型和基准上取得最优的污染缓解效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准测试数据不可避免地泄露到预训练语料中，导致评估分数被夸大。当前污染缓解评估的常用指标G-AP存在根本性缺陷：离散的正确/错误判读无法刻画每题级别的表现；先平均再差分会使过度抑制和抑制不足相互抵消；均匀加权会诱导策略将求解概率推向干净模型的高频值。因此需要更可靠的评估指标和更有效的缓解策略。

Method: 1. SA-PPG指标：通过采样估计每道题的求解概率，与干净模型逐题差分，然后按干净模型的求解概率进行分层聚合。2. RailCap策略：在生成过程中实时判断污染——当样本回落到贪心轨迹时，将下一个token限制为次优选择，累积抑制直到响应分布充分分散。

Result: SA-PPG揭示出先前策略的恢复效果被显著高估；RailCap在多个受污染模型和基准上取得了最低的SA-PPG值。

Conclusion: G-AP作为污染缓解评估指标不可靠，SA-PPG提供了更精确的每题级别概率差异度量；RailCap通过生成时实时判断和逐步抑制的方式，无需预先估计污染位置，实现了更优的污染缓解效果。

Abstract: Test data from public benchmarks inevitably leaks into pretraining corpora, inflating evaluation scores once memorized. \textbf{Contamination mitigation evaluation} intervenes in the decoding process to suppress memorization and restore a contaminated model's genuine capability, but its prevailing metric, the \textbf{G-AP} (\textbf{G}ap of \textbf{A}ggregate \textbf{P}erformance), is flawed. Discrete correct/incorrect readouts cannot characterize per-question performance, averaging before differencing lets over- and under-suppression cancel out, and uniform per-question weighting invites strategies to push solve probabilities onto the clean model's high-frequency values. We propose \textbf{SA-PPG} (\textbf{S}tratified \textbf{A}ggregate of \textbf{P}er-question \textbf{P}robability \textbf{G}aps): estimate each question's solve probability by sampling, difference it against the clean model per question, and aggregate within groups defined by the clean model's solve probability. Existing mitigation strategies first estimate where contamination lies and then operate on the estimate, so they are only as correct as the estimate. \textbf{RailCap} instead judges contamination during generation: whenever a sample falls back onto the greedy trajectory, the next trajectory token is capped to the runner-up, accumulating suppression until the response distribution becomes sufficiently dispersed. Across multiple contaminated models and benchmarks, SA-PPG reveals that prior strategies' restoration is substantially overestimated, while RailCap attains the lowest SA-PPG.

</details>


### [120] [Geo-Spatial Concept Probing of Large Language Models: Abstraction, Compositionality, and Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07353)
*Karim Radouane,Jose G Moreno,Lynda Tamine*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文设计了一个以概念为核心的基准测试，通过空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）的问答任务，系统性地探测LLM在抽象性、组合性和基础性三个核心属性上的概念理解能力，揭示了当前LLM在结构化概念获取与组合方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM概念理解的方法要么使用自然语言基准（混杂多种技能），要么使用范围狭窄的合成任务（缺乏对概念及其属性的精确控制），因此需要一种能够精确控制概念属性、进行系统性探测的评估框架。

Method: 作者围绕概念的三个核心属性——抽象性（abstraction）、组合性（compositionality）和基础性（groundness）设计测试，构建了一个以空间概念（方向、距离、拓扑及其组合）为目标的基准，采用问答任务作为代理评估，并在多种LLM架构和训练范式上进行了广泛实验。

Result: 实验结果揭示了当前LLM在概念理解上的明显局限，同时分析了模型规模和设计如何影响概念理解能力，为理解LLM获取和组合结构化概念的关键因素提供了洞见。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了LLM在结构化概念理解方面的不足，并指出未来可基于概念重新设计LLM，以改进信息访问和知识管理能力。

Abstract: Understanding concepts is fundamental to generalization. Despite their impressive performance on a wide range of tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with genuine concept understanding. Prior work has evaluated conceptual understanding in LLMs using natural-language benchmarks or narrowly scoped synthetic tasks, but these settings often conflate multiple skills or lack precise control over the underlying concepts and their properties. To support controlled probing of concepts in LLMs, we design tests on their core properties: abstraction, compositionality, and groundness. We set up a concept-centric benchmark, targeting spatial concepts such as direction, distance, topology, and their compositions, and use question answering tasks serving as a proxy. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple LLM architectures and training regimes to analyze how model scale and design impact conceptual understanding. The results reveal clear limitations in current LLMs and provide insights into the factors shaping their ability to acquire and compose structured concepts. Our findings shed light on how concept-based LLMs can be redesigned for improved information access and knowledge management. The code will be available at https://github.com/rd20karim/concept-probing.

</details>


### [121] [LitTraceQA: A Benchmark for Multi-Stage Grounding and Verification in Scientific Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07370)
*Xuye Liu,Yimu Wang,Peng Shi,Bo Xue,Xiangrui Ke,Songcheng Cai,Kath Choi,Di Wu,Freda Shi,Krzysztof Czarnecki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LitTraceQA 是一个面向科学文献的问答基准，要求系统在给定研究问题后，同时输出论文标识、证据定位和答案，覆盖表格、图表、文本片段、公式/算法及引用上下文等多种证据类型，支持对论文检索、证据支撑和答案准确性进行分离评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学文献问答系统往往只追求生成流畅的回答，缺乏对答案可追溯性和证据忠实性的要求。亟需一个基准来评估系统能否精确定位相关论文、找到具体证据并生成忠实于证据的答案。

Method: 构建 LitTraceQA 基准，给定研究问题和论文元数据池，要求系统输出三类关联结果：标准论文标识符、支持证据位置（表格、图表、文本片段、公式/算法、引用上下文）、以及在自由文本、多选题、结构化表格等格式下的答案。开发集含55个示例（26道单论文题、29道多论文题），并提供黄金论文、证据标注和答案供本地验证。最终标注集合含4,978条唯一问题记录，覆盖4,859篇唯一黄金论文。

Result: LitTraceQA 将论文检索、证据定位和答案准确性作为三个独立维度进行评测，构建了一个可验证答案的评估框架，而非仅评估无依据的摘要生成。

Conclusion: LitTraceQA 为科学文献问答系统提供了严格的可验证性基准，推动研究从表面流畅的生成向有据可查、可追溯的科学问答范式转变。

Abstract: Scientific literature is increasingly used as a knowledge source for language models, retrieval-augmented generation systems, and research assistants, but answering research questions from papers requires more than fluent generation. A reliable system must identify the relevant papers, locate the concrete evidence that supports the answer, and produce a response that is faithful to that evidence. We present LitTraceQA, a benchmark for literature-grounded question answering over scientific papers. Given a research question and a metadata pool of papers, a system must return three connected outputs: canonical paper identifiers, supporting evidence locations, and answers in one or more requested formats, including free-form text, multiple-choice answers, and structured tables. LitTraceQA targets evidence types common in scientific reading: tables, figures, text spans, equations or algorithms, and citation contexts. The public development split contains 55 examples, including 26 hidden-source single-paper questions and 29 multi-paper questions, and provides gold papers, evidence annotations, and answers for local validation. We also analyze a larger final annotation collection with 4,978 unique-question records over 4,859 unique gold papers. By evaluating paper retrieval, evidence grounding, and answer accuracy separately, LitTraceQA provides a testbed for scientific QA systems that produce verifiable answers rather than unsupported summaries.

</details>


### [122] [An Exploratory Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Rewriting of Moderate-Complexity Financial Sentences for DisCoCat-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07439)
*Brian Llinas,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM辅助的文本重写预处理流程，通过压缩和简化中等复杂度的金融情绪语句，使其适配DisCoCat量子自然语言处理框架。实验表明，GPT-4.1-mini配合特定提示策略可将电路量子比特和门数减少超70%，准确率达0.550±0.035，优于基线0.521±0.050。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DisCoCat框架在金融情绪分析中存在解析器敏感性高、模拟成本大、难以处理长句等实际局限，需要通过LLM辅助重写使中等复杂度语句也能被高效处理。

Method: 采用LLM辅助预处理工作流，通过受控重写对中等复杂度金融情绪句进行压缩、简化或分解；比较不同提示策略、语言模型和过滤配置，在电路层面评估量子比特和门数减少效果，并通过重复训练实验测量分类准确率。

Result: 最强压缩变体将平均量子比特和门数降低超70%；GPT-4.1-mini配合Prompt B达到最高平均准确率0.550±0.035，优于基线0.521±0.050；训练集大小与准确率呈中度负相关（Pearson r=-0.446）。

Conclusion: LLM辅助重写可使部分中等复杂度输入在DisCoCat配置下可用，提示设计、过滤和电路感知预处理是实现更可扩展的QNLP金融情绪分析的关键考量因素。

Abstract: Quantum natural language processing (QNLP) provides a grammar-aware framework for text modeling, and Distributional Compositional Categorical (DisCoCat) is one of its theoretically grounded formulations. Prior work on financial sentiment analysis has identified practical limitations of DisCoCat, including parser sensitivity, high simulation cost, and difficulty handling longer sentences. We study an LLM-assisted preprocessing workflow that uses controlled rewriting to compress, simplify, or decompose moderate-complexity financial sentiment sentences into parser-compatible, circuit-efficient variants while preserving sentiment-bearing meaning. We compare prompting strategies, language models, and filtering configurations with the low-complexity-only DisCoCat baseline of Stein et al. At the circuit level, the strongest compression variants reduce average qubit and gate counts by more than 70 percent relative to the raw moderate-complexity subset. Across repeated training runs, GPT-4.1-mini with Prompt B achieves the highest observed mean accuracy, $0.550 \pm 0.035$, compared with $0.521 \pm 0.050$ for the baseline. Larger training splits do not necessarily improve downstream performance; across evaluated configurations, training-split size has a moderately negative association with accuracy (Pearson $r=-0.446$). These results provide exploratory evidence that LLM-assisted rewriting can make some moderate-complexity inputs usable within the evaluated DisCoCat configuration, while highlighting prompt design, filtering, and circuit-aware preprocessing as considerations for more scalable QNLP-based financial sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [123] [CoinRAG: Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07458)
*Gyuwan Kim,Cheoneum Park,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoinRAG 提出了一种细粒度的 KV 缓存复用方法，通过两阶段检索识别查询相关的语义单元（nuggets），并将其切片 KV 表示与块级上下文组合，在低预填充延迟约束下实现了 RAG 的 Pareto 前沿优化，在 LongBench 多跳问答任务上取得了平均 5.3% 的 F1 相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 优化工作利用块级（chunk-level）KV 缓存复用来加速长上下文处理，但粗粒度块中仍存在大量信息冗余和噪声，导致在严格的延迟预算下难以兼顾效率与精度。因此，需要一种更细粒度、语义更相关的 KV 缓存复用机制，以在低预填充延迟约束下实现更优的精度-效率权衡。

Method: CoinRAG 的核心机制是将离线计算的细粒度 "nugget"（语义单元）缓存进行组合式复用。具体而言，它通过两阶段检索在已检索块中识别与查询相关的语义单元；然后，不进行全块编码，而是将这些语义单元的切片 KV 表示与块级上下文无缝组装，以更紧凑且语义相关的方式高效构建学习到的上下文表示。

Result: 在 LongBench 多跳问答任务上的广泛评估表明，CoinRAG 显著降低了运行成本，在标准快速预填充延迟预算下，相较其他基线方法构建了新的 Pareto 前沿，并在答案质量（F1）上取得了平均 5.3% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: CoinRAG 通过细粒度 nugget 级别的 KV 缓存组合式复用，成功在低延迟约束下实现了 RAG 精度与效率的 Pareto 最优，验证了从粗粒度块到细粒度语义单元的粒度下移对长上下文 RAG 优化的有效性。

Abstract: Recent optimization studies on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have exploited chunk-level KV cache reuse to avoid processing long retrieved contexts for higher efficiency, while significant information redundancy and noise still remain in the coarse-grained chunks. This paper optimizes the Pareto frontier under low prefill latency constraints while maximizing accuracy by proposing CoinRAG (Contextualized Information Nugget KV Cache Reuse for Long-Context RAG). The name metaphorically reflects our core mechanism: much like assembling small tokens (or "coins") to accumulate a larger value, CoinRAG compositionally reuses offline-computed, fine-grained nugget caches to form a learned contextual representation efficiently in a more semantically relevant but compact manner. Specifically, instead of full-chunk encoding, CoinRAG identifies query-relevant semantic units within retrieved chunks through two-stage retrieval and seamlessly assembles their sliced KV representations with a chunk-level context. Extensive evaluations on LongBench multi-hop question answering tasks demonstrate that CoinRAG significantly reduces operational costs and outperforms the other baselines with a new Pareto frontier and an average 5.3% relative improvement in answer quality (F1) under a standard fast prefill latency budget.

</details>


### [124] [CreativeInstruct: Scalably Teaching LLMs to Balance Quality, Creativity, and Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07460)
*Ananya Sahu,Mohit Bansal,Elias Stengel-Eskin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CreativeInstruct 是一种可扩展的指令微调方法，通过注入特殊的 [StartCreativity] 标记来教 LLM 在创意生成与后训练质量之间取得平衡，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了输出多样性，并且在强化学习场景中也能带来收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练虽然提升了 LLM 的能力，但普遍降低了输出的多样性和创造力，这对显式需要创造力的任务（如故事生成）和隐式需要创造力的任务（如强化学习探索）都造成了负面影响。现有的多样性提升方法往往需要多个模型或牺牲生成质量，缺乏一种单一模型即可兼顾质量与多样性的方案。

Method: 提出 CreativeInstruct，一种指令微调方法，通过在训练中学习插入 [StartCreativity] 标记来引导模型偏向创意/基础模型风格的生成。同时引入基于图编辑距离（graph edit distance）的结构多样性指标，用于捕捉纯词汇和语义指标所遗漏的叙事层面变化。

Result: 在叙事生成任务上，CreativeInstruct 在多样性上匹配或超越了多模型基线和蒸馏变体，且不牺牲质量，推理时也无需多个模型。人工评估中，70.3% 的情况下标注者认为 CreativeInstruct 的生成比后训练 LLM 更有创造力。在强化学习中，对 CreativeInstruct 检查点应用 GRPO 后，AMC 提升约 4%，MATH 提升约 5 个百分点，优于直接在后训练检查点上进行相同训练。

Conclusion: CreativeInstruct 证明了通过指令微调可以在单一模型内实现创造力与质量的平衡，既保留了后训练模型的输出质量，又恢复了基础模型的多样性，同时还能作为强化学习的优质基座，为创意生成和探索型任务提供了新的思路。

Abstract: While post-training improves the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), it generally lowers their output diversity and creativity, negatively impacting tasks that explicitly require creativity (e.g., story generation) as well as those that require it implicitly, e.g., reinforcement learning (RL). We instead propose CreativeInstruct, a scalable instruction-tuning method that teaches LLMs to balance creative, base-model-like generations with the quality of post-trained models, by learning to inject special [StartCreativity] spans that bias generation toward creativity. Furthermore, we introduce a structural diversity metric based on graph edit distance, which captures narrative level variation missed by purely lexical and semantic metrics. On narrative generation, CreativeInstruct matches or exceeds the diversity of both multi-model baselines and distilled variants of their outputs, without sacrificing quality or requiring multiple models at inference time. These results are mirrored in our human evaluation, where we find that annotators rate CreativeInstruct generations as more creative than the post-trained LLMs' generations in 70.3% of cases. We also show the benefits of creative models as a substrate for RL: GRPO applied to a CreativeInstruct checkpoint improves by ~4% on AMC and ~5% points on MATH over the same training applied to the post-trained checkpoint.

</details>


### [125] [Mitigating Scoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge via Random Number Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05726)
*Yuma Asato,Kiyoaki Shirai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM-as-a-Judge中存在的评分偏差问题，提出了一种通过随机生成数字令牌来测量LLM潜在数字偏差，并在评估时对生成概率进行校正的去偏方法，在四个任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为评估器（LLM-as-a-Judge）虽优于传统基于参考文本的自动评估指标，但存在评分偏差（scoring bias）——即LLM倾向于生成特定分数而忽略被评估文本的上下文。现有校准方法未能充分考虑LLM在不同任务和评分区间中的潜在数字偏好差异。

Method: 首先，通过让LLM随机生成数字令牌，测量其输出数字分布与均匀分布的偏差，以此识别LLM的潜在数字偏差（latent numerical bias）。进一步，在下游任务的提示中加入任务定义，测量任务特定的潜在数字偏差。在实际评估时，利用测得的潜在数字偏差对给定输入下的令牌生成概率进行校正（rectification），从而消减评分偏差。

Result: 在LLM对齐评估、摘要评估、语义文本相似度（STS）和语义文本相关性（STR）四个任务上的实验表明，该方法优于无去偏的LLM评估器及先前的校准方法。此外，实验证实评分偏差因LLM、任务类型和评分区间的不同而存在差异。

Conclusion: 通过测量和校正LLM的潜在数字偏差，可以有效缓解LLM-as-a-Judge中的评分偏差问题。偏差随LLM、任务和评分区间变化，说明因地制宜地测量潜在数字偏差具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are often used as evaluators of text quality, known as LLM-as-a-Judge, which can outperform conventional automatic evaluation metrics that rely on reference texts. However, LLM evaluators tend to generate particular scores regardless of the context of the evaluated text, which is known as scoring bias. This study proposes a novel method to mitigate this scoring bias. An LLM is instructed to randomly generate number tokens, and the latent numerical bias of the LLM is identified by measuring the deviation of the observed distribution of numbers from the uniform distribution. A definition of a downstream task, for which an LLM evaluator is used, is added to the prompts for random number generation to measure task-specific latent number bias. In the evaluation by an LLM, the token generation probabilities for a given input are rectified considering the LLM's latent number bias. Results of the experiment on four different tasks, evaluation of LLM alignment, evaluation of summarization, Semantic Textual Similarity, and Semantic Textual Relatedness, demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms the baselines, including an LLM without debiasing and previous calibration methods. In addition, it is confirmed that scoring bias varies across LLMs, tasks, and score ranges, indicating the importance of measuring latent number bias as the case may be.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Robust Average-Reward Markov Decision Processes: Minimax-Optimal Learning via Plug-in Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06545)
*Yuepeng Yang,Yuxin Chen,Yuejie Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平均奖励准则下，分布鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（DR-MDP）中学习ε-最优鲁棒策略的样本复杂度。针对生成模型和(s,a)-矩形TV不确定性集，作者给出了匹配的上下界，揭示样本复杂度在高低容忍度区间存在相变现象，并由σH₀刻画分界，并提出了基于归约的即插即用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒MDP为模型不确定性下的序列决策提供了原则性框架，但现有工作多集中于折扣准则，平均奖励准则下鲁棒策略学习的样本复杂度尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，刻画学习鲁棒策略所需的最小样本量。

Method: 采用生成模型获取名义转移核的样本，在(s,a)-矩形总变差（TV）不确定性集下评估策略性能。通过归约方法（reduction-based plug-in），将鲁棒平均奖励问题归约到名义或鲁棒的折扣问题，设计span-informed和span-agnostic两种算法，分别利用已知span参数和从数据中校准参数来选择归约类型与折扣因子。理论分析给出匹配的极小极大上下界。

Result: 样本复杂度为 N S A ≍ (SA/ε²) · Φ，其中Φ在ε ≳ σH₀时为min{H₀, H_σ}，在ε ≲ σH₀时为min{H₀, H_σ} + σH_σ²。σH₀为高/低容忍度区间的相变边界。低容忍度区间额外出现鲁棒性特有项σH_σ²，高容忍度区间则退化为名义AMDP的线性span项。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了分布鲁棒平均奖励MDP的样本复杂度呈现出由σH₀分隔的相变结构：高容忍度时与名义MDP无异，低容忍度时需额外支付鲁棒性代价。所提归约算法实现了匹配下界的上界，表明该方法是极小极大最优的。

Abstract: Distributionally robust Markov decision processes provide a principled framework for sequential decision making under model uncertainty. We study how many samples are necessary and sufficient to learn an $\varepsilon$-optimal robust policy under the average-reward criterion. A generative model provides samples from the nominal transition kernel, whereas policy performance is evaluated over $(s,a)$-rectangular total-variation uncertainty sets of radius at most $σ$.
  Let $H_0$ and $H_σ$ denote the nominal and robust optimal bias spans, respectively. We identify $σH_0$ as the perturbation scale separating high- and low-tolerance regimes. Our matching upper and lower bounds show that, up to logarithmic factors, the minimax total sample complexity is $$ NSA \asymp \frac{SA}{\varepsilon^2}\begin{cases} \min\{H_0,H_σ\}, & \varepsilon\gtrsimσH_0,\\ \min\{H_0,H_σ\}+σH_σ^2, & \varepsilon\lesssimσH_0. \end{cases} $$ Here $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, and $N$ is the number of samples per state-action pair. The sample complexity consists of a linear-span term that resembles the nominal AMDP results and a robustness-specific term that appears only in the low-tolerance regime. We attain these rates using reduction-based plug-in procedures that select the reduction---nominal or robust---and its discount factor: a span-informed procedure that makes these choices using known span parameters, and a span-agnostic procedure that calibrates both choices from data.

</details>


### [127] [Omni-modal decomposition autoencoders learn full-stack wearable disentangled representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07385)
*Ioannis Ziogas,Ensieh Khazaei,Bilal Taha,Aamna Al Shehhi,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Dimitrios Hatzinakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniDecVAEs 是一个统一的框架，通过模态条件化的时频潜在子空间和多视图自监督分解损失，从任意多模态时序数据中学习解耦表示，在活动识别、身份识别、数据生成和重建上全面超越 Transformer 和 VAE 基线方法，且保持轻量化（4.1M 参数）和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态可穿戴计算方法无法同时满足任务分类性能、解耦可解释表示学习、多模态融合与生成建模的全栈处理需求，尤其面对高达数十种高度异构的模态时缺乏统一且可扩展的方案。

Method: 提出 OmniDecVAEs 框架，扩展 DecVAEs：(1) 引入模态条件化的时频潜在子空间；(2) 设计多视图自监督分解损失函数；(3) 采用共享的非对称自编码器架构，以统一可扩展方式处理任意数量的模态。

Result: 在多达30种模态的人体活动识别任务上，活动识别准确率提升1.01%，身份识别准确率提升6.75%；重建MAE降低76.84%；合成数据与真实数据的MMD改善13.85%。模型仅4.1M参数，支持实时推理。

Conclusion: OmniDecVAEs 在单一轻量模型中统一了全栈可穿戴处理能力，具备增强的表示容量、模态不变的空间复杂度和实时延迟，适合智能边缘可穿戴设备与临床医疗应用。

Abstract: Learning disentangled representations is a key requirement for developing versatile, general-purpose, and sustainable models in multi-modal wearable computing. However, existing approaches do not operate as full-stack wearable processors, i.e., they do not simultaneously address task-specific classification performance, disentangled and interpretable representation learning, fusion, and generative modeling of highly heterogeneous multi-modal time series. To address this gap, we introduce Omni-modal Variational Decomposition Autoencoders (OmniDecVAEs), a framework that efficiently learns multi-purpose representations in a unified and scalable manner from arbitrarily many modalities. OmniDecVAEs extend DecVAEs by learning modality-conditioned time-frequency latent subspaces through a multi-view self-supervised decomposition loss and a shared asymmetric autoencoder (AE) architecture. Results on a challenging omni-modal human activity recognition (HAR) setting with up to thirty modalities, demonstrate the ability of OmniDecVAEs to learn full-stack wearable representations. When compared to transformer-based and VAE-based methods, OmniDecVAEs full-stack disentangled representation properties lead to accuracy improvements of 1.01% and 6.75% in activity and identity recognition, respectively. Furthermore, OmniDecVAEs synthesize realistic omni-modal time-frequency data that manifest with enhanced reconstructions (mean absolute error improves by 76.84%) and distributional similarity between real and synthetic data (maximum mean discrepancy improves by 13.85%). Our results highlight OmniDecVAEs potential as a lightweight model suitable for intelligent edge wearables and clinical healthcare, unifying processing requirements and abilities in a single model, through its enhanced representational capacity, modality-invariant spatial complexity (4.1M parameters), and real-time latency.

</details>


### [128] [Latent Fact-Checking: Detecting Misinformation through Activation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06417)
*Pedro Barcelos,Otávio Parraga,Marcelo M. Mussi,Lucas M. Fraga,Lucas S. Kupssinskü,Rodrigo C. Barros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活工程的 misinformation 检测框架，通过在残差流中对比真假陈述的激活差异来提取"虚假方向"，进而对未见声明的最后 token 激活进行投影并用 MLP 分类，无需微调、无需外部知识检索，在多个模型家族和基准上验证了真值性作为潜在空间中线性可分几何属性的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数虚假信息检测方法依赖表层语言特征或外部知识检索，本文试图从语言模型表征空间的几何结构出发，探究"真值性"是否可作为模型潜在空间中的一个线性可分离方向，从而开辟一种可解释性驱动的检测范式。

Method: 采用对比激活添加（CAA）的差均值原则：首先用成对的真实与虚假陈述分别前向传播，收集残差流激活，计算两者的均值差作为"虚假方向"。推理时，提取未见声明的最后一个 token 的残差流激活，将其投影到该方向上，然后将投影后的表征输入一个多层感知机（MLP）进行分类。整个过程无需微调骨干模型，也不需要外部证据或任务特定监督。

Result: 在 Gemma、Llama、Qwen 三个家族、参数规模从 270M 到 12B 的 11 个模型上，于 AVeriTeC、LIAR 和 FACTors 三个事实核查基准上评估。虚假方向在跨模型尺度和架构家族上均可恢复；在 LIAR 和 FACTors 上，最后 token 投影方法匹配或超越零样本/少样本提示基线，小模型增益最显著；在 AVeriTeC 上表现受限，作者归因于其证据标注机制。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 真值性是预训练语言模型潜在空间中一个结构化、线性可分的概念；基于可解释性的虚假信息检测可作为检索式管线的实用补充方案。

Abstract: The proliferation of misinformation online has driven demand for scalable detection systems. While most existing approaches rely on surface-level linguistic features or external knowledge retrieval, we examine truthfulness as a geometric property of a language model's representation space. We introduce a misinformation detection framework grounded in activation engineering, which leverages the latent geometry of transformer models. Our approach elicits a misinformation direction in the residual stream by contrasting activations from paired truthful and false statements, following the difference-in-means principle of Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA). At inference time, the last-token activation of an unseen claim is projected onto this direction, and the projected representation is fed to an Multilayer Perceptron (MLP) for classification. The procedure requires no fine-tuning of the backbone model, no external evidence retrieval, and no task-specific supervision beyond the contrastive pairs used to estimate the direction. We evaluate the method across 11 models from the Gemma, Llama, and Qwen families, ranging from 270M to 12B parameters, on three fact-checking benchmarks: AVeriTeC, LIAR, and FACTors. The falsehood direction is recoverable across model scales and architectural families, and last-token projection matches or surpasses zero-shot and few-shot prompting baselines on LIAR and FACTors, with the largest gains observed for smaller models. Performance on AVeriTeC is more limited, which we attribute to its evidence-grounded labeling scheme. These findings provide evidence that truthfulness is a structured, linearly separable concept in the latent space of pretrained language models, and point toward interpretability-driven misinformation detection as a practical complement to retrieval-based pipelines. The code is available on https://github.com/Malta-Lab/LaFaCt.

</details>


### [129] [Risk-Aware Decision Policies for Agents Under Noisy Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06420)
*David Szczecina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个噪声感知下的捕食者-猎物人工生命模型，比较了不同不确定性处理策略的智能体表现。结果表明，盲目信任感知标签在噪声增加时会导致灾难性失败，而采用不确定性感知策略能显著提升生存率并减少致命错误，且智能体行为会从探索型向保守型转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物感知系统固有地存在噪声，而误分类代价可能致命。现有研究中缺乏对噪声感知下决策策略的系统比较，作者旨在通过人工生命模型揭示不确定性感知在觅食决策中的关键作用，并建立风险敏感觅食、生态信息使用与人工生命之间的桥梁。

Method: 构建人工生命捕食者-猎物觅食模型，在对称和非对称感知噪声两种条件下进行受控实验。比较多种策略：盲目信任感知标签的策略与多种考虑预测不确定性的策略（不确定性感知策略），通过生存率和致命错误率等指标评估智能体表现。

Result: （1）盲目信任策略随噪声增加导致灾难性失败；（2）不确定性感知策略显著提升生存率、减少致命错误；（3）随不确定性增加，智能体行为出现质性相变，从探索型策略转向保守型策略；（4）显式信息收集可在感知不可靠时提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知决策对在噪声环境中的鲁棒性至关重要。该模型为带噪声标签的鲁棒学习提供了可解释的人工生命类比，强调了将不确定性纳入决策过程的重要性。

Abstract: Perception in biological systems is inherently noisy, requiring organisms to make decisions under uncertainty where misclassification can be costly or fatal. We present an Artificial Life predator-prey model of foraging under noisy perception, and compare agent performance when using various policies that take into account their noisy predictions. Through controlled experiments under both symmetric and asymmetric perceptual noise, we show that blindly trusting perceptual labels leads to catastrophic failure as noise increases, while uncertainty-aware strategies significantly improve survival and reduce fatal errors. We further observe qualitative regime shifts in behaviour, with agents transitioning from exploratory to conservative strategies as uncertainty increases. Our model links risk-sensitive foraging, ecological information use, and Artificial Life by showing that explicit information gathering can improve robustness when perception is unreliable. These results highlight the importance of uncertainty-aware decision-making and provide an interpretable artificial life analogue to robust learning with noisy labels.

</details>


### [130] [Sharding Prevents LLM Oversight Failures and Adversarial Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06422)
*Victor Akinwande,J. Zico Kolter,Aran Nayebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当单个LLM评判调用需要同时返回大量裁决时，其判断质量会下降——与专家的一致性随每次调用裁决数量增加而降低。作者提出"分片"（Sharding）方法：将需求拆分为更小组、分派给独立调用、再聚合裁决。在保持模型、证据、总预算和单决策预算不变的情况下，分片能提升一致性，使较弱的评判者超越更强的整体性评判者，同时对对抗性攻击表现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评判者（LLM judge）在单个调用中需要返回多项裁决时，部分决策的证据基础会变薄弱，即使该调用拥有与多个独立调用相同的token或工具预算。这导致在高风险领域（如科研复现评审、法律工作、临床试验评估）中与专家的一致性下降。作者旨在揭示这一"过载"问题，并提出有效干预措施。

Method: 提出"分片"（Sharding）方法：将待评估的需求集合划分为更小的子组，每个子组分配给独立的LLM调用，最后聚合各组的裁决结果。实验设计上，在保持模型、证据材料、总计算预算、以及每条需求的平均预算均不变的前提下，对比单次整体调用与分片调用的表现。此外，还测试了分片在面对"Best-of-N"对抗攻击（只改变呈现方式、不改变底层工作内容）时的鲁棒性，以及在逐条说服场景中叠加辩论式对抗（debate-style opposition）的效果。

Result: 1. 与专家一致性随每次调用裁决数量增长而下降；2. 分片在相同总预算下提升一致性，分片后的较弱评判者可超越更强的整体性评判者，并与之匹敌；3. 分片对Best-of-N对抗攻击具有鲁棒性——当分片降低了基线错误时，即消除了对手通过改变呈现方式使不达标项被错误接受的放大效应；4. 对于逐条说服（而非利用过载）的攻击场景，分片本身不足以应对，但在分片之上叠加辩论式对抗可以有效抵御此类自适应重优化攻击。

Conclusion: 分片是一种简单而有效的干预手段，能在不增加总预算的前提下缓解LLM评判者的"过载"问题，提升模型化监督（model-based oversight）的可靠性。分片不仅提升了与专家的一致性，还提供了对抗性鲁棒性。对于分片无法覆盖的攻击形式，辩论式对抗可作为补充防御。

Abstract: Giving an LLM judge more compute does not necessarily make it check more requirements. When one call must return many verdicts, some decisions become weakly grounded in the evidence, even when that call receives the same token or tool budget as a panel of separate calls. Across expert-graded research replications, legal work, and clinical-trial assessments, agreement with experts falls as the number of verdicts per call grows. We identify sharding as the intervention that mitigates this failure in model-based oversight. Sharding partitions the requirements into smaller groups, assigns each group to a separate call, and aggregates the verdicts. Against a single call with the panel's full budget, sharding improves agreement while holding the model, evidence, total budget, and per-decision budget fixed. Overall, we find that a sharded weaker judge can outperform a more capable holistic judge and match that judge even when the latter receives the panel's full budget. Additionally, we find that sharding exhibits robustness against adversaries. A best-of-N adversary can hold the underlying work fixed, vary only its presentation, and increase an overloaded judge's acceptance of genuinely unmet criteria severalfold. Wherever sharding reduces baseline error, it removes this adversarial advantage, keeping over-acceptance low even as the adversary's search widens. Sharding does not address attacks that persuade the judge separately on each criterion rather than exploiting overload. In that setting, we find that debate-style opposition on top of sharding withstands such adaptive re-optimization.

</details>


### [131] [Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06427)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性因果干预证伪（ACIF）框架，将因果结构学习建模为结构因果生成器与对抗性实验者之间的序贯博弈，其中实验者通过选择干预来最大化证伪生成器的后干预分布，从而区分观测等价但因果结构不同的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型可以完美拟合观测分布却编码错误的因果结构。现有方法常混淆三个概念：观测拟合、容许查询类上的干预等价性、以及结构因果模型的点识别。需要一个统一框架来澄清对抗性因果判别器能认证什么、不能认证什么。

Method: 构建ACIF博弈：结构因果生成器提出观测与干预分布，对抗实验者选择干预以最大化分布差异（最差干预积分概率度量）。区分三种对象（观测拟合、干预等价性、点识别），分析有限模型与干预类下的理论性质，并通过线性高斯示例验证。

Result: 证明五项理论结果：(1)对抗目标可精确归约为最差干预积分概率度量；(2)在分离干预族下实现点识别，否则识别至干预等价类；(3)混合策略均衡存在性；(4)有限样本一致收敛与基于margin的模型选择保证；(5)平衡分离条件下基于分歧驱动的序贯设计的对数消除保证。

Conclusion: ACIF框架澄清了对抗性因果判别器的认证边界，在因果生成建模、主动因果发现和实验设计之间架起了原则性桥梁。单个精心选择的干预即可分离观测上不可区分的因果方向。

Abstract: Generative models can reproduce an observational distribution while encoding an incorrect causal structure. We study a sequential game in which a structural causal generator proposes observational and interventional distributions, while an adversarial experimentalist selects interventions intended to maximally falsify the generator. The discriminator is therefore not merely a real-versus-synthetic classifier: it is indexed by an intervention and tests whether the generator reproduces the corresponding post-intervention law. We introduce Adversarial Causal Intervention Falsification (ACIF), formulate oracle and implementable versions of the game, and distinguish three objects that are often conflated: observational fit, interventional equivalence over an admissible query class, and point identification of a structural causal model. For finite model and intervention classes, we prove: (i) an exact reduction of the adversarial objective to a worst-intervention integral probability metric; (ii) identification up to interventional equivalence, with point identification under a separating intervention family; (iii) existence of mixed-strategy equilibria; (iv) finite-sample uniform convergence and margin-based model-selection guarantees; and (v) a logarithmic elimination guarantee for a disagreement-driven sequential design under a balanced-separation condition. We also give a complete linear-Gaussian example in which two observationally indistinguishable causal directions are separated by a single well-chosen intervention. The framework clarifies what an adversarial causal discriminator can and cannot certify, and provides a principled bridge between causal generative modeling, active causal discovery, and experimental design.

</details>


### [132] [Fixed and Adaptive Topological DeepONets: Functional Measurements on Hausdorff Locally Convex Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06428)
*Khemraj Shukla,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG
