To bridge this gap, we propose Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling (TOFD), a unified framework that jointly enables proactive detection and robust optimization against a wide range of poisoning attacks. TOFD operates in three stages: (1) Target Inference, which identifies potential attack targets by refining class-wise safe zones via class-specific Margin Perturbation (MP); (2) Sample Purification, which adaptively filters poisoned smashed data using thresholds calibrated through cross-class min-max normalization of MP; and (3) Decoupling Optimization, which leverages an adversarial guidance model to capture attack-induced patterns and decouple their influence during optimization, thereby suppressing residual adversarial effects. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of TOFD. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that TOFD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses under diverse attack scenarios, achieving superior robustness with low computational overhead suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [199] [A foundation-model approach to pediatric headache classification from rs-fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07287)
*Guilherme S. Imai Aldeia,Clara Moon,Julie Shulman,Navil Sethna,Allison Smith,Alyssa Lebel,William G. La Cava,Scott Holmes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用静息态功能MRI和基础模型NeuroSTORM，结合机器学习对儿童头痛进行检测与亚型分类，在区分头痛与非头痛方面AUROC达0.82，优于传统功能连接方法，但在区分非慢性头痛亚型方面仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头痛是儿童最常见的神经系统疾病，严重影响生活质量。目前缺乏基于客观影像标志物的儿童头痛分类方法，该研究旨在探索静息态fMRI结合机器学习是否可辅助儿童头痛的诊断与亚型分类。

Method: 使用189例rs-fMRI扫描（来自110名受试者，两次访视），采用NeuroSTORM基础模型编码fMRI数据进行微调，与基于功能连接矩阵的传统方法对比。任务包括：(1)区分头痛与健康对照的二分类；(2)健康对照、慢性偏头痛与非慢性头痛的三分类。评估指标为AUROC和AUPRC。

Result: NeuroSTORM在头痛vs非头痛任务中AUROC=0.82、AUPRC=0.93，显著优于FC矩阵方法（AUROC=0.67，AUPRC=0.85）。三分类任务中宏平均AUROC=0.69，能较好区分慢性偏头痛，但难以区分其他头痛亚型与慢性偏头痛。

Conclusion: 在有限数据条件下，NeuroSTORM能够捕捉潜在的rs-fMRI表征并迁移至头痛相关任务，无需依赖FC特征。该研究为基于fMRI预测儿童头痛提供了概念验证，未来有望用于亚型识别和个体化治疗策略。

Abstract: Headache is the most common neurological disorder in children and substantially affects quality of life. We investigated whether resting-state functional MRI (rs-fMRI) can support pediatric headache classification using machine learning. We encoded rs-fMRI data using NeuroSTORM, a recent foundation model, and fine-tuned it to distinguish healthy controls from children with headache and subsequently classify headache subtypes. We compared NeuroSTORM with a standard neuroscience approach using functional-connectivity (FC) matrices derived from brain activity as predictors. Using 189 rs-fMRI scans from 110 individuals collected across two visits (prevalence of any headache: 74%), NeuroSTORM achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.82 (95% CI, 0.82-0.82) and an area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) of 0.93 (95% CI, 0.93-0.94) for discriminating headache from non-headache. In contrast, models trained on FC matrices showed lower performance (AUROC, 0.67 [95% CI, 0.67-0.67]; AUPRC, 0.85 [95% CI, 0.85-0.85]). In multiclass classification of healthy controls, chronic migraine, and non-chronic headaches (e.g., post-viral headache, new daily persistent headache, post-traumatic headache), NeuroSTORM achieved a macro-AUROC of 0.69 (95% CI, 0.68-0.69). Results suggest that the approach can distinguish chronic migraine but has difficulty differentiating other headache subtypes from chronic migraine. Overall, under limited-data conditions, NeuroSTORM appears to capture latent rs-fMRI representations that transfer to headache-related tasks without relying on FC features. These findings provide proof of concept for fMRI-based prediction of pediatric headache and highlight potential future utility for subtype identification and individualized treatment strategies.

</details>


### [200] [FUSE: Feature-Wise Unified Specialization with Cross-Column Exchange for Mixed-Type Tabular Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07294)
*Suman Cha,Seongchan Lee,Dohyun Ko,Hyunjoong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FUSE 提出了一种混合类型表格数据生成框架，通过为数值型和类别型特征分别设计自适应混合模块（特征级统一专业化）并辅以跨列联合注意力机制（跨列交换），将特征专业化处理与列间交互显式分离，同时在理论上刻画了受限条件上下文带来的超额人口风险，并在八个数据集上验证了其在分布保真度和下游效用上的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变分流匹配方法虽然通过因子化分布处理混合类型数据的不同端点，但将特征特定处理与跨列交互隐式地耦合在共享骨干网络中，导致建模效率不足。需要一种能够显式分离特征专业化与列间信息交换的框架。

Method: 1. **FUSE 框架**：将特征特定处理与跨列交互显式解耦。2. **自适应混合模块**：为数值型和类别型特征分别设计独立的自适应混合模块，每种特征可以组合共享的专业化子网络。3. **联合注意力机制**：通过跨所有列的联合注意力保持信息交换。4. **理论分析**：刻画受限条件上下文导致的超额人口风险，并将连续 Wasserstein 生成误差上界归约为端点预测风险。

Result: 在八个表格数据集上的综合实验表明，FUSE 在分布保真度（distributional fidelity）和下游效用（downstream utility）两个维度上均取得了强且一致的性能表现。

Conclusion: FUSE 通过特征级统一专业化与跨列交换的显式分离，有效提升了混合类型表格数据的生成质量，理论分析与实验结果均验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Generating mixed-type tabular data requires jointly modeling diverse feature distributions and their complex cross-column dependencies. Variational flow matching handles distinct endpoints via factorized distributions, yet leaves feature-specific processing and cross-column interactions implicit within a shared backbone. We introduce Feature-wise Unified Specialization with cross-column Exchange (FUSE) to explicitly separate these roles. FUSE applies separate adaptive mixture modules to numerical and categorical features, allowing each feature to combine shared specialized subnetworks, while joint attention preserves information exchange across all columns. We also characterize the excess population risk from restricted conditioning contexts and bound the continuous Wasserstein generation error by endpoint-prediction risk. Comprehensive experiments on eight tabular datasets demonstrate that FUSE achieves strong and consistent performance across distributional fidelity and downstream utility metrics.

</details>


### [201] [$\boldsymbolλ$-Orthogonality Regularization for Compatible Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16664)
*Simone Ricci,Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Ioannis Patras,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在仿射变换学习中施加λ-正交正则化的方法，旨在解决不同检索模型表示空间的对齐问题，既实现分布特定的适应性，又保留原始已学习的表示结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索系统中模型更新后的表示空间需要与旧模型对齐，但现有两种主流方法各有缺陷：仿射变换适应性好但会显著改变原始表示结构，正交变换能保持几何结构但适应性受限。需要一种能兼顾两者优势的方法。

Method: 在仿射变换的学习过程中施加松弛的正交约束，称为λ-Orthogonality正则化。通过引入超参数λ控制正交约束的强度，在保持原始表示结构和适应下游分布之间取得平衡。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上的广泛实验验证了该方法的有效性：既能保持模型的零样本（zero-shot）性能，又能确保模型更新间的表示兼容性。

Conclusion: λ-正交正则化提供了一种简单而有效的方式来调和表示适应性与结构保持之间的矛盾，在检索模型的持续更新中具有实用价值。代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval systems rely on representations learned by increasingly powerful models. However, due to the high training cost and inconsistencies in learned representations, there is significant interest in facilitating communication between representations and ensuring compatibility across independently trained neural networks. In the literature, two primary approaches are commonly used to adapt different learned representations: affine transformations, which adapt well to specific distributions but can significantly alter the original representation, and orthogonal transformations, which preserve the original structure with strict geometric constraints but limit adaptability. A key challenge is adapting the latent spaces of updated models to align with those of previous models on downstream distributions while preserving the newly learned representation spaces. In this paper, we impose a relaxed orthogonality constraint, namely $λ$-Orthogonality regularization, while learning an affine transformation, to obtain distribution-specific adaptation while retaining the original learned representations. Extensive experiments across various architectures and datasets validate our approach, demonstrating that it preserves the model's zero-shot performance and ensures compatibility across model updates. Code available at: \href{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda_orthogonality.git}{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda\_orthogonality}.

</details>


### [202] [From Optimal Actions to World Models: Identifiability of Transition Kernels in Discounted MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07301)
*Neal Batra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅从最优动作中能恢复多少关于MDP转移概率的信息。作者证明：两个核对所有奖励给出相同最优动作的充要条件是通过一个特定矩阵变换关联；在严格正核附近存在n(n-1)维的不可区分族。进一步比较了状态-动作奖励、仅状态奖励、以及依赖下一状态的奖励三种形式下可恢复信息的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Letcher等人研究了从数值Q值中恢复动态的问题。本文进一步限制：连数值都不可观测，仅知道每一类奖励下的最优动作。这对应于观察智能体行为但不获取其内部估值的情形，核心动机是探讨在极端信息匮乏条件下，动态模型的不可区分性边界。

Method: 作者采用代数分析方法，将"对所有奖励产生相同最优动作"的条件转化为核之间的等价关系。核心推导得到等价条件：Q_{s,a} = (P_{s,a} + 1/γ e_s^T (L-I)) L^{-1}，其中L为满足L1=1的可逆矩阵。通过分析该变换的自由度，计算不可区分族维度。进而对比r(s,a)、r(s)、r(s,a,s')三种奖励形式下的可恢复性。

Result: （1）两个核对所有状态-动作奖励产生相同最优动作，当且仅当存在满足L1=1的可逆矩阵L使上述等式成立。（2）在严格正核附近，存在n(n-1)维的不同核族不可区分。（3）即使限制为每状态有唯一最优动作的奖励，结果不变。（4）依赖下一状态的奖励通常可恢复转移核本身：至少有两个动作的状态各行被确定；仅有一个动作的状态行可能保持隐藏。（5）状态奖励揭示的信息最少：两核等价当且仅当每个确定性策略对相同奖励集是最优的。

Conclusion: 奖励函数的形式深刻影响从最优动作中可学习的动态信息量。r(s,a,s')几乎完全可识别转移核，r(s)可识别性最弱，r(s,a)介于中间。这为逆强化学习和行为克隆中的可辨识性理论提供了精确的代数刻画。

Abstract: We study what can be recovered about the transition probabilities of a Markov decision process from optimal actions alone. This is closely related to the inverse problem considered by Letcher et al., who ask when the dynamics can be recovered from numerical \(Q\)-values. Here the numerical values themselves are not observed; only the optimal actions are known, for every reward in a given class.
  For state-action rewards \(r(s,a)\), knowing the optimal actions for every reward also tells us how much better one action is than another when each is followed by the same fixed policy. This is still not enough to determine the transition probabilities uniquely. We prove that two kernels give the same optimal actions for every reward exactly when \[ Q_{s,a} =
\Bigl(P_{s,a}+\tfrac1γe_s^{\mathsf T}(L-I)\Bigr)L^{-1} \] for one invertible matrix \(L\) satisfying \(L\mathbf 1=\mathbf 1\). Near a kernel with strictly positive entries, there is an \(n(n-1)\)-dimensional family of different kernels with this property. The result is unchanged if we consider only rewards having a unique optimal action at every state.
  We then compare this with rewards of the forms \(r(s)\) and \(r(s,a,s')\). Rewards that depend on the next state can usually recover the transition kernel itself: every row at a state with at least two actions is determined, and we describe exactly when a row at a state with one action can remain hidden. State rewards reveal less: two kernels give the same optimal actions exactly when every deterministic policy is optimal for the same set of rewards. The results show how the form of the reward affects what can be learned about the dynamics from optimal actions alone.

</details>


### [203] [Is SwiGLU's Open Positive Tail Necessary? Evidence from Closed-Tail Gating with MemGLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07323)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了SwiGLU激活函数中开放正尾部在解码器仅语言模型FFN中的必要性，提出MemGLU作为闭尾部对比方案。在9M和30M规模的预训练实验中，MemGLU的验证NLL与SwiGLU差距在0.1%以内，表明在该规模下SwiGLU的开放正尾部并非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究解码器仅语言模型的前馈网络是否真正需要SwiGLU激活函数的开放正尾部特性。SwiGLU因其门控线性单元设计在Transformer FFN中被广泛采用，但其正半轴无上界（open positive tail）是否对模型性能起关键作用尚不明确。

Method: 提出MemGLU作为闭尾部对比激活函数，其设计灵感来源于忆阻器分支几何结构。在9M和30M参数规模、三种随机种子条件下进行成对预训练实验，对比SwiGLU与MemGLU的验证负对数似然（NLL）。同时，通过正尾部抑制实验测试已训练SwiGLU检查点的敏感性，并利用机制诊断分析两种模型的门控使用模式差异。

Result: MemGLU在验证NLL上与SwiGLU的差距保持在约0.1%以内，性能接近。已训练的SwiGLU检查点对正尾部抑制表现出敏感性，而机制诊断显示尽管损失相近，两种模型使用门控的方式存在差异。这表明模型会根据预训练时可用的门几何结构进行自适应调整。

Conclusion: 在测试的规模下（9M和30M），SwiGLU的开放正尾部对于解码器仅语言模型的FFN并非必要，模型能够适应不同的门控几何结构并取得相近的性能。

Abstract: We test whether decoder-only language-model FFNs require SwiGLU's open positive tail. We introduce MemGLU as a closed-tail comparator derived from a memristive branch geometry. Across paired 9M and 30M pretraining runs with three seeds, MemGLU remains within about 0.1% of SwiGLU in validation NLL. Trained SwiGLU checkpoints are sensitive to positive-tail suppression, while mechanism diagnostics show that the two models use their gates differently despite similar losses. These results suggest that models adapt to the gate geometry available during pretraining. At the tested scales, SwiGLU's open positive tail is not necessary for decoder-only language-model FFNs.

</details>


### [204] [When GNNs Fail: Quantifying and Overcoming Temporal Correlation Volatility in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07333)
*Chen Shao,Yue Wang,Zhenyi Zhu,Zhanbo Huang,Tobias Käfer,Zonghan Wu,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了GNN在多元时序预测中因忽略时序相关性动态演化(TCV)而性能退化的问题，并提出GLIDE层，通过路径消息传递与静动态传播分离两大机制，在静态和动态场景下均显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN方法假设静态图拓扑，忽略了多元时间序列中节点间相关性随时间剧烈演化的现象。作者观察到在高动态场景下，主流模型(包括Transformer)性能严重退化，甚至不如忽略结构的基线模型，因此需要形式化这一现象并提出解决方案。

Method: (1) 提出TCV指标：一个模型无关的度量，用于量化时序相关性的分布演化程度；(2) 提出GLIDE层：包含两个核心设计——D1路径消息传递(Path-based Message Passing)，捕获基于路径的邻域信息；D2静动态传播分离(Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation)，通过局部静态近似识别最优动态模式。

Result: 在合成和真实基准数据集上的大量实验表明，GLIDE在静态和动态场景下平均性能提升达45.6%，最大提升达85.7%。同时验证了高TCV与性能退化之间的明确关联。

Conclusion: TCV有效量化了动态图结构对预测的影响，GLIDE通过双机制设计在保持静态鲁棒性的同时大幅提升动态拓扑下的学习能力，为动态图时序预测提供了新的SOTA方案。

Abstract: Modeling multivariate time series by representing them as graphs, where individual series act as nodes and pairwise temporal corre- lations serve as edges, has gained significant traction. Recent advances in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated strong perfor- mance by assuming a static graph topology and aggregating information from neighboring series. In this work, we investigate the representa- tional power of GNNs for forecasting under both static and dynamic settings (i.e., when pairwise correlations evolve drastically over time) and identify critical limitations in current architectures. To formalize this, we first propose Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV), a model- agnostic metric designed to quantify the distributional evolution of these latent structures. We establish a clear connection between TCV and performance degradation, demonstrating that many popular models, including Transformers, generalize poorly in high-TCV settings and are often outperformed by simple structure-agnostic baselines. To address these limitations, we propose Graph Layer for Inference in Dynamic En- vironments (GLIDE), a novel GNN layer enhanced by two theoretically grounded design mechanisms: (D1) Path-based Message Passing, which captures path-based neighborhoods and (D2) Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation, which identifies optimal dynamics via local static approximation. These components significantly improve learning under dynamic topology while preserving robustness in static scenarios. Ex- tensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GLIDE improves average performance by up to 45.6% across static and dynamic settings, with the largest gain reaching 85.7%. The source code is available at https://github.com/ChenS676/GLIDE.

</details>


### [205] [Aftab: A Comprehensive Benchmark of CNN Encoders and Advanced Value Functions in Parallelized Q-Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07335)
*Taha Shieenavaz,Shabnam Zareshahraki,Loris Nanni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了在无重放缓冲区、无目标网络的并行化Q网络（PQN）框架下，CNN视觉编码器的架构设计空间。作者设计并评估了八种CNN拓扑，并整合Hadamax编码范式及分布式、集成、对决头等Q-learning扩展，提出复合架构Aftab。在Atari-57基准上，Aftab的IQM人类标准化得分达6.479，相比PQN基线有0.86的改进概率；在Procgen Hard基准上也展现出更强的分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度强化学习趋向于简化、高度并行化的范式（如PQN），但在此类无缓冲区设定下，视觉编码器的表示能力和参数效率尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，寻找在严格参数约束下实现高效采样的CNN架构设计。

Method: 系统设计并严格评估八种不同的CNN拓扑结构；引入Hadamax编码范式增强表示学习；集成分布式Q-learning、集成方法和对决头等高级Q-learning扩展；在Atari-57和Procgen Hard两个基准上进行大规模实验验证，同时评估结构韧性。

Result: 所提出的Aftab架构在Atari-57上达到IQM人类标准化得分6.479，相较于标准PQN基线的改进概率为0.86；在Procgen Hard上IQM归一化得分0.418（基线0.382），验证了分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为无模型强化学习建立了一个高效、概率上显著优越的结构参考，在保持无缓冲区并行优化简洁性与内存效率的同时，提升了采样效率和泛化性能。完整框架已开源。

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep reinforcement learning have increasingly favored simplified, highly parallelized paradigms. Notably, the Parallelized Q-Network (PQN) algorithm achieves stable off-policy learning without relying on computationally expensive replay buffers or target networks. However, the representational capacity and parameter efficiency of visual encoders operating in these buffer-free settings remain underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the architectural design space of Convolutional Neural Networks for PQN. We design and rigorously evaluate eight distinct CNN topologies, optimizing for sample efficiency under strict parameter constraints. Furthermore, we study the impact of representation and value estimation enhancements by integrating the Hadamax encoding paradigm and advanced Q-learning extensions, including distributional, ensemble, and dueling heads. Extensive experiments on the Atari-57 benchmark demonstrate that our proposed composite architecture, Aftab, achieves an Interquartile Mean (IQM) Human-Normalized Score of 6.479, establishing a 0.86 Probability of Improvement over the standard PQN baseline. Additionally, structural resilience evaluations on the highly non-stationary Procgen Hard benchmark confirm out-of-distribution generalization, with Aftab yielding an IQM Procgen Normalized Score of 0.418 compared to the baseline's 0.382. Ultimately, this work establishes an efficient, probabilistically superior structural reference for model-free reinforcement learning, all while preserving the simplicity and memory efficiency of unbuffered, parallelized optimization.
  The complete Aftab framework, including all model definitions, training configurations, and raw experimental logs, is open-sourced and available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/tahashieenavaz/aftab

</details>


### [206] [Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07349)
*Yao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对异构表示学习中残差进行代数化的方法，将表示视为带有坐标系的类型对象，通过Fold将表示实现为条件均值场，通过FPRC-PQ实现relax-aggregate-close代数组合，在370万A股数据上实现了显著的风险调整收益提升，超越了同等容量的控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异构表示学习通常简化为特征拼接（feature concatenation），但这会抹去"哪个表示产生了误差"这一信息，即抹除了表示的"身份"（identity）和残差归属（residual ownership）。作者认为应当保留表示的"类型"信息，让每个表示对自己的残差负责，从而实现更精细、更透明的组合学习。

Method: 作者将表示代数化为带类型的对象：每个表示拥有自身的坐标系及其未解决的残差。方法分为两部分：(1) Fold：将表示具体实现为10×10等级网格上的时点条件均值场（point-in-time conditional-mean fields）；(2) FPRC-PQ代数：采用"放松-聚合-闭合"（relax-aggregate-close）三步操作——每个场用自身残差拟合的修正项进行放松；修正后的场在固定均值处汇合，该均值是唯一的身份擦除边界；共享学习器仅闭合聚合后新产生的残差。该组合精确地伸缩分解为：表示本身、局部残差估计、残差之残差估计。聚合结果构成一个带总体方差缩减的学习型控制变量接口（control-variate interface），而对闭合器沿骨干扰动进行重拟合可获得一阶耦合路径均值正交性。此外还引入反思性"反刍算子"（rumination operator），读取全局重构相对于聚合锚的位移，进行反射并通过唯一正交投影（而非网格搜索）固定增益。

Result: 在370万条中国A股日度数据（2023-2026）上采用冻结时点协议评估：基础代数将净收益率从13.52%提升至19.10%，Sharpe比率从1.42提升至2.09。同等容量控制、统一残差控制、无身份两阶段控制和仅成对控制均显著落后。实验表明，收益提升并非来自更多特征或更多树，而是源于在表示身份尚可获取时对残差归属和组合的显式建模。

Conclusion: 显式保留表示的"类型身份"及其残差归属，并通过代数化的放松-聚合-闭合组合框架进行学习，能够显著优于传统的特征拼接或身份擦除式集成方法。该方法的有效性在真实大规模金融数据上得到实证验证，其优势源于结构性的代数设计而非简单的容量扩充。

Abstract: Learning from heterogeneous representations is usually reduced to feature concatenation, which erases which representation produced an error. We instead algebraize the residual: a representation is a typed object that owns both a coordinate system and the residual it leaves unresolved, and learning is an ordered composition of operators that preserve or deliberately erase that type. Fold realizes the objects as point-in-time conditional-mean fields on 10x10 rank grids. FPRC-PQ realizes the algebra as relax-aggregate-close: each field is relaxed by a correction fitted to its own residual in its own coordinates; corrected fields meet at a fixed mean that is the sole identity-erasure boundary; and a shared learner closes only the aggregate's fresh residual. The composition telescopes exactly into representation, local residual estimate, and residual-of-residual estimate. Its aggregate is a learned control-variate interface with population variance reduction, while refitting the closer along perturbations of the backbone yields first-order coupled-path mean orthogonality. As an analytical extension, a reflective rumination operator reads the displacement of a global reconstruction from the aggregate anchor, reflects it, and fixes its gain by a unique orthogonal projection rather than return-tuned grid search. On 3.67M Chinese A-share stock-day observations (2023-2026) under a frozen point-in-time protocol, the evaluated base algebra raises net-of-cost return from 13.52% to 19.10% and Sharpe from 1.42 to 2.09. Matched-capacity, unified-residual, identity-free two-stage, and pairwise-only controls all trail it. The gain is therefore not explained by more features or more trees, but by making residual ownership and composition explicit while representation identity is still available.

</details>


### [207] [FedDOSE: Federated Learning Framework Decomposing Site Effects for Modeling Brain Dynamic Functional Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07393)
*Deepank Girish,Yi Hao Chan,Yubin Zheng,Sukrit Gupta,Jagath C. Rajapakse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedDOSE 是一个联邦学习框架，通过显式分解站点差异来分析动态功能连接（dFC）。其核心创新包括：模组引导的 Tucker 分解模块用于高效编码高维 dFC 张量，以及结合最优传输（OT）重心和 Procrustes 分析的类别原型全局对齐策略。在 ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II 和 ADHD-200 三个多站点静息态 fMRI 数据集上的 ASD 和 ADHD 诊断实验中，FedDOSE 超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多站点 fMRI 数据整合面临两大挑战：(1) 联邦学习中站点间的统计异质性（站点差异）影响模型泛化；(2) 现有联邦学习方法仅依赖静态功能连接（FC），忽略了大脑网络的动态信息。因此需要一种能够显式处理站点差异并有效利用动态 FC 的联邦学习框架。

Method: 提出 FedDOSE 框架，包含两个关键组件：(1) Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition 模块——将高维 dFC 张量编码为低维表示，高效捕获模块级别的时空模式；(2) 全局原型对齐策略——首先生成各站点的类别特定原型，然后通过最优传输（OT）重心公式与 Procrustes 分析相结合，在全局层面实现跨站点原型对齐，从而显式分解并缓解站点差异。

Result: 在三个多站点静息态 fMRI 数据集（ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II、ADHD-200）上进行自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）和注意力缺陷多动障碍（ADHD）诊断实验，FedDOSE 在 ASD 和 ADHD 检测任务上均超越了当前最先进的方法，证明其在多站点数据上学习鲁棒表示的有效性。

Conclusion: FedDOSE 通过显式分解站点差异并结合动态功能连接分析，有效解决了联邦学习中多站点 fMRI 数据的统计异质性问题，在多种神经发育障碍诊断中展现出优越的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging ( fMRI ) data are often pooled into collaborative multi-site consortia, as deep learning models for analyses require large datasets to generalize well. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative training, standard approaches continue to struggle with statistical heterogeneity. In particular, site differences pose a key challenge in multi-site data settings. Additionally, existing FL approaches for fMRI rely on static Functional Connectivity ( FC), omitting dynamic information in brain networks. To address this, we propose FedDOSE, a novel framework that explicitly decomposes site differences for analysis of dynamic FC (dFC). FedDOSE introduces a Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition block to encode high-dimensional dFC tensors and capture modular-level spatio-temporal patterns efficiently. Class-specific prototypes are generated across all sites and subsequently aligned at the global level by using a combination of Optimal Transport (OT) barycenter formulation and Procrustes analysis. Extensive experiments for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) and Attention-Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) on three multi-site resting-state fMRI datasets: ABIDE-I, ABIDE-II, and ADHD-200, demonstrate that FedDOSE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in ASD and ADHD detection. Our results highlight its effectiveness in learning robust representations from multi-site datasets for reliable analysis.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Post-Hoc Temperature Scaling: Bilevel Optimization for LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07419)
*Ruochen Jin,Zhanliang Wang,Zongyu Dai,Jiancong Xiao,Bojian Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在训练阶段通过最大化预测分布熵来改善大语言模型校准的方法，采用双层优化框架，使用一阶近似实现高效训练，在域外泛化方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐（如RLHF）常导致LLM过度自信、校准不良。传统温度缩放是后处理手段且高度依赖领域，无法跨域泛化。因此需要在训练阶段直接修改模型参数以改善校准。

Method: 提出以最大化预测分布熵作为校准目标，通过双层优化实现：下层在参数化损失下训练模型，上层选择损失超参数以最大化熵。为适应LLM规模，采用一阶近似避免显式二阶计算。

Result: 在多项选择题和开放式生成问答任务上，方法能产出校准良好的LLM，尤其在域外泛化方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过在训练中直接优化预测熵，可有效缓解偏好对齐带来的过度自信问题，实现跨域鲁棒的模型校准，且方法在LLM规模上具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Preference alignment often makes large language models (LLMs) overconfident and poorly calibrated. Traditional post-hoc temperature scaling is inherently domain-dependent: a temperature fitted on one domain does not generalize across domains. This motivates us to modify model parameters during training to improve calibration. We propose maximizing the entropy of predictive distributions as the calibration objective, which directly targets overconfidence by discouraging overly concentrated predictions. Inspired by temperature scaling, we realize this through a bilevel optimization formulation, where the lower level trains the model under a parametric loss and the upper level selects loss hyperparameters to maximize entropy. To make the framework practical at LLM scale, we adopt an efficient first-order approximation that avoids explicit second-order computation. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended generative question answering, experiments demonstrate that our method yields well-calibrated LLMs with particular advantages in out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Myopic World Models: Long-Horizon End-to-End Training for Direct Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07420)
*Xinyi Li,Zaishuo Xia,Chenjie Hao,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 世界模型通常通过局部少步预测训练，却需递归推演长时域，导致误差放大。本文提出直接预测世界模型（DPWM），通过端到端的终点预测目标，将任意长度动作序列压缩为单一嵌入并一次性预测终点观测，避免递归推演。实验表明DPWM在长时域预测上显著优于递归基线，且证明训练目标而非模型架构是长时域精度的主要驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型存在根本性失配：训练时使用局部少步预测损失优化局部转移保真度，但部署时需递归推演长时域。这导致两个问题：（1）训练中对终点影响不同的转移被同等对待；（2）局部小误差在递归推理中被逐步放大。作者认为应通过端到端的终点预测目标直接优化长时域精度。

Method: 提出直接预测世界模型（DPWM），一种非递归架构。核心设计：将任意长度的动作序列压缩为单一嵌入向量，通过单次前向传播直接预测终点观测。该设计在预测和梯度传播中均避免了递归展开，使得在递归自回归训练变得不稳定的长时域上，端到端训练仍可实际进行。

Result: 在连续控制和像素基准上，DPWM的长时域终点预测显著优于递归世界模型基线，且优势随预测时域增加而扩大。进一步将递归基线用相同的长时域终点目标重训练后，其表现同样显著提升，证明训练目标是长时域预测精度的主要驱动力，而非特定的骨干架构。

Conclusion: 世界模型应在其最终被使用的时域尺度上进行训练和评估，应从关注局部转移建模转向关注长时域预测精度。端到端的终点预测目标比递归少步训练更适合长时域任务。

Abstract: World models are expected to support imagination over extended temporal horizons, yet most are still trained through local few-step prediction objectives and deployed by recursively rolling out their own predictions. This creates a fundamental mismatch: few-step losses optimize local transition fidelity, while long-horizon prediction depends on how errors and gradients propagate through the entire trajectory. As a result, transitions with different downstream influence on the endpoint are treated uniformly during training, and small local errors are amplified through recursive inference. We argue that long-horizon accuracy is better achieved by optimizing directly, through an end-to-end endpoint prediction objective. To instantiate this paradigm, we introduce the Direct Prediction World Model (DPWM), a non-recursive architecture that compresses an action sequence of arbitrary length into a single embedding and predicts the endpoint observation in a single forward pass. This design avoids recurrent rollout in both prediction and gradient propagation, making long-horizon end-to-end training practical at horizons where unrolled autoregressive training becomes unstable. Empirically, DPWM substantially improves long-horizon endpoint prediction over recursive world-model baselines on continuous-control and pixel-based benchmarks, with larger gains as the prediction horizon increases. We further show that recurrent baselines benefit similarly when retrained with the same long-horizon endpoint objective, supporting our central claim that the training objective, rather than the particular backbone choice, is the main driver of long-horizon prediction accuracy. Our results suggest that world models can benefit from being trained and evaluated at the temporal scales where they are ultimately used, shifting the focus from local transition modeling toward long-horizon predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [210] [Diffusion LLMs as Targets and Adversaries: Mechanistic Safety Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07430)
*Elena Dumitrescu,Gert Lek,Lydia Y. Chen,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）的安全对齐漏洞，发现其安全机制稀疏且可跨架构迁移，进而提出了一种名为SN-Guided Diffusion的黑盒越狱框架，以极低的生成成本实现了高成功率的迁移攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLLMs采用迭代并行去噪替代自回归预测，但其内部安全机制尚不清楚。作者旨在揭示扩散模型在对齐方面的机制性漏洞，既将其作为攻击目标，也将其作为攻击其他模型的跳板。

Method: 首先通过安全神经元映射与剪枝，证明DLLMs继承了自回归前身模型的安全机制足迹，可实现跨架构迁移攻击。在此基础上提出SN-Guided Diffusion：一种完全离线的黑盒越狱框架，利用加权安全神经元损失引导扩散过程远离安全触发区域，仅需每个提示20次生成迭代即可产生越狱样本。

Result: 自剪枝使LLaDA的ASR从2.6%升至73.8%，Dream从1.9%升至86.6%；从Qwen2.5迁移剪枝使Dream的ASR从1.9%升至73.2%，Fast-dLLM从7.0%升至86.3%。SN-Guided Diffusion在良性/越狱判别上达到AUROC=1.0，迁移攻击ASR在Llama-3-8B-Instruct达77.1%，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct达86.9%，Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite达74.3%，且生成成本比现有方法低数个数量级。

Conclusion: DLLMs的安全对齐存在稀疏性和可迁移性等机制性漏洞，其安全机制继承自自回归前身模型。SN-Guided Diffusion作为一种高效的黑盒越狱方法，以极低的计算开销实现了对多个开源和商业模型的强迁移攻击能力，揭示了扩散模型安全对齐的脆弱性。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) replace autoregressive next-token prediction with iterative parallel denoising, yet their internal safety mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate DLLMs both as targets and as adversaries, exposing mechanistic vulnerabilities in diffusion-based alignment.
  We first show that safety alignment in DLLMs remains sparse and transferable across architectures. DLLMs initialized from autoregressive predecessors inherit the same mechanistic safety footprint as their source models, enabling transfer attacks via direct safety neuron mapping and pruning. Self-pruning increases attack success rates (ASR) from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA and from 1.9% to 86.6% on Dream, while transfer pruning from Qwen2.5 increases ASR from 1.9% to 73.2% on Dream and from 7.0% to 86.3% on Fast-dLLM.
  Building on these findings, we introduce SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that steers the diffusion process away from safety-triggering regions using a weighted safety neuron loss, which achieves near-perfect prompt separability (AUROC = 1.0 for benign-vs-jailbreak discrimination). Across multiple open and proprietary targets, our method achieves a transfer ASR of up to 77.1% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, 86.9% on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and 74.3% against Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, while requiring only 20 generation episodes per prompt. Compared to prior jailbreaking frameworks, our method achieves competitive transferability with orders-of-magnitude lower generation cost.
  Our codebase is available at https://github.com/ellyoana/sn-guided-diffusion.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Bayesian Distilled Clustering for High-Dimensional Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06509)
*Pulkita Aggarwal,Abhishek Bhattacharjee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高维混合模型的贝叶斯精馏聚类框架。该框架通过贝叶斯变量选择估计后验包含概率，筛选对子群分离有实质性贡献的协变量，构建"精馏"子空间后进行聚类，并通过条件独立性诊断检验子群内变量依赖结构。该方法将降维与混合模型结构直接关联，确保所得子空间与识别潜在异质子群的科学目标保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维场景下，驱动潜在子群分离的关键协变量往往只占少数，大量无关变量引入噪声和冗余。传统聚类方法对所有维度等权处理，导致无关坐标扭曲距离度量，掩盖定义潜在类别的低维结构。现有降维方法多基于通用准则，而非直接服务于混合模型中的子群识别目标。

Method: 提出贝叶斯精馏聚类框架：(1) 利用贝叶斯变量选择模型估计每个协变量的后验包含概率，量化其对子群分离的贡献证据；(2) 通过控制期望错误发现比例，识别"精馏"协变量集合；(3) 在降维子空间上执行聚类；(4) 对所选变量进行条件独立性诊断，检验子群内变量间的依赖结构。该框架基于模型驱动，精馏步骤直接与混合模型结构和响应模型绑定。

Result: （摘要中未提供具体实验结果或数值结论，仅阐述了方法框架的设计逻辑与预期优势。）

Conclusion: 该框架通过将子空间选择与混合模型结构直接关联，克服了通用降维准则与聚类目标脱节的问题，为高维混合模型中的潜在子群分析提供了统计上合理的解决方案。精馏后的子空间保持了与科学目标——即识别在分布结构和行为上均存在差异的潜在子群——的一致性。

Abstract: Latent subgroup analysis is central to fields such as genomics, precision medicine, and social science, where the goal is to identify heterogeneous populations with distinct covariate structures or response behaviors. Mixture models provide a natural probabilistic framework for this task, representing the data-generating distribution as a weighted combination of subgroup-specific laws with unobserved labels. In high-dimensional regimes, these analyses face significant challenges. Often, only a small subset of covariates drives meaningful subgroup separation; the remaining variables may introduce noise or redundancy. Standard clustering methods typically treat all dimensions as equal, but in high-dimensional spaces, irrelevant coordinates can distort distances and obscure the low-dimensional structures defining latent classes. This paper introduces a Bayesian distilled clustering framework for high-dimensional mixture models. We propose that clustering should occur within a statistically justified subspace rather than the full ambient space. Our method utilizes a Bayesian variable selection model to estimate posterior inclusion probabilities, quantifying the evidence that each covariate contributes to subgroup separation or response behavior. A "distilled" covariate set is then identified by controlling the expected false-discovery proportion. Clustering is performed on this reduced subspace, followed by conditional independence diagnostics to examine subgroup-specific dependencies among selected variables. Critically, this framework is model-based: the distillation step is tied directly to the mixture structure and response model rather than a generic dimension-reduction criterion. This ensures the resulting subspace remains aligned with the scientific objective: identifying latent subgroups that differ in both distributional structure and behavior.

</details>


### [212] [A Latent Trajectory Analysis for Multivariate Outcomes with Mixed-Scale: Application to Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06514)
*Lindsay R. Salvati,Jungwun Lee*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对混合类型多元纵向数据的潜轨迹模型，利用EM算法进行估计，可识别个体层面的时变潜类别归属及潜轨迹归属，并在ADNI阿尔茨海默病数据上进行了应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床研究中，患者的疾病进程或治疗反应往往存在显著异质性，传统分析方法将患者进行平均化处理，掩盖了重要的亚组差异。现有方法难以同时处理多元纵向结局的混合数据类型（连续、二分类、计数等）并刻画潜类别随时间动态变化的轨迹。

Method: 提出一种新颖的潜轨迹模型，可处理混合类型的多元纵向结局变量。模型能够同时识别：(1) 个体在每个时间点的潜类别归属（time-specific latent class membership）；(2) 描述潜类别随时间演变规律的潜轨迹归属（latent trajectory membership）。模型采用期望最大化（EM）算法进行参数估计。

Result: 该方法应用于阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）数据，这是一项用于验证阿尔茨海默病生物标志物的多中心纵向观察性研究。通过捕捉患者结局的动态变化，模型能够识别高风险患者、揭示改善或恶化的早期指标，为个体化治疗规划提供支持。

Conclusion: 所提出的潜轨迹模型能有效处理混合类型多元纵向数据，揭示患者群体中隐藏的异质性轨迹，为临床个体化决策提供了有力的统计工具。ADNI应用表明该方法在识别疾病进展模式方面具有实用价值。

Abstract: Latent trajectory analysis is a statistical method for explaining heterogeneity by partitioning patients into homogeneous subgroups based on similarities in outcome variables. In the context of clinical work, patients often do not follow the same course of illness or treatment response, and traditional analyses often average across patients, masking important subgroups. This work proposes a novel latent trajectory model for multivariate longitudinal outcomes with mixed types and uses the expectation-maximization algorithm as an estimation strategy. The proposed model can identify individual-level, time specific latent class memberships and a latent trajectory membership that describes how the latent class memberships change over time. By capturing these dynamic changes, we can highlight patients at higher risk of poor outcomes, reveal early indicators of improvement or decline, and ultimately support more individualized treatment planning. We present an application of our methodology to the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), a longitudinal, multi-center, observational study to validate biomarkers for Alzheimer disease (AD) clinical trials.

</details>


### [213] [Mixed Gaussian Projections for Two-Sample Testing of Functional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06542)
*Fernando A. Najman,Marcela Svarc*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机投影的函数数据检验方法，通过混合高斯测度生成投影方向，结合Haar与Fourier成分分别捕捉局部化和振荡性差异，并引入数据自适应的池化函数主成分协方差算子以提升检验效力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数数据的随机投影检验高度依赖于投影方向的概率分布选择。传统方法仅使用单一高斯测度，难以同时有效检测局部化偏离和振荡性偏离，且有限样本下的检验效力受投影分布支配，需要更具针对性和适应性的投影策略。

Method: 作者提出：(1) 使用非退化高斯测度的混合来定义投影分布，证明其保持几乎必然分离性质并诱导函数概率律间的度量；(2) 通过集中不等式揭示检验效力由积分投影距离决定；(3) 具体构造融合Haar（局部化）和Fourier（振荡性）高斯成分的混合投影；(4) 提出池化函数主成分分析协方差算子作为有限秩、数据自适应的投影几何，通过标签不变构造保持置换有效性。

Result: 模拟实验表明Haar与Fourier成分各自具有专门化的检测能力，其混合具有鲁棒性。ECG5000数据集应用识别出两类间以局部化差异为主的差异模式。池化协方差算子在多个模拟设置下——尤其是协方差结构变化情形——显著提升了检验效力。

Conclusion: 混合高斯投影测度与数据自适应投影几何为函数数据双样本检验提供了灵活且高效的框架，既能保持统计一致性，又能通过成分标签和上尾分离分数对检测到的差异几何结构提供描述性解释。

Abstract: Random-projection tests for functional data depend on the probability law used to generate projection directions. A measure has to be selected to generate the random directions in which the data is projected. In $L^2$, probability measures defined by their moments can be discriminated using Gaussian measures. The covariance operator of the Gaussian projection law determines which regions and structures of the functional space receive appreciable probability, and consequently affects finite-sample power. We show that mixtures of non-degenerate Gaussian measures preserve the almost-sure separation property and induce a metric between functional probability laws. A concentration argument further makes explicit that the power of projection tests is governed by the integrated projected distance associated with the chosen law. As a concrete construction, we combine Haar and Fourier Gaussian components, which emphasize localized and oscillatory departures, respectively. The resulting permutation test retains consistency, while component labels and upper-tail separation scores provide a descriptive indication of the geometry of the detected discrepancy. Simulations illustrate the specialization of the two components and the robustness of their mixture, and an ECG5000 application identifies a predominantly localized difference between two classes. We also compare with a pooled functional principal component analysis covariance operator. This finite-rank, data-adaptive projection geometry preserves permutation validity through its label-invariant construction and improves power in several simulated settings, particularly under a change in covariance structure.

</details>


### [214] [An Entropy-based Coefficient of Determination with Adjustment of Optimization Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06624)
*Longhai Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出熵方差（Entropic Variance, EV）作为OLS误差方差的尺度无关推广，定义$[0,1]$标准化的$ρ^2_V$参数，推导其渐近F分布性质，构建两个估计量$R^2_{\text{SV}}$和$R^2_{\text{SVP}}$，并通过模拟和帕金森病微生物组应用验证其在变量选择中可将错误发现率从80%降至6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典似然比检验和ΔAIC因随样本量缩放而加剧显著性危机；ATE等因果估计量受限于原始坐标尺度；现有伪$R^2$忽视高阶分布变化且缺乏单调变换不变性。亟需一种尺度无关、具有分布理论支撑的拟合优度度量。

Method: 1) 定义人口层面的熵方差参数$ρ^2_V$，将无界交叉熵投影到$[0,1]$标准化尺度；2) 证明EV-based $F_\text{V}$统计量渐近服从F分布；3) 构造两个估计量：经验人口$R^2_{\text{SV}}$和样本外预测$R^2_{\text{SVP}}$，通过对逐观测交叉熵取指数并引入自由度校正控制训练乐观偏差；4) 利用$F_\text{V}$分布推导$ρ^2_V$的精确p值和置信区间，避免难以处理的Fisher信息矩阵。

Result: 模拟研究表明，通过数据分割评估LASSO路径的$R^2_{\text{SVP}}$，将错误发现率从80%降至6%，同时完全保留信号召回率。帕金森病微生物组应用进一步验证了EV-$R^2$度量在变量选择中的优越性。

Conclusion: 熵方差框架提供了一种尺度无关、具有完整分布理论的拟合优度评估范式，有效缓解传统检验的显著性危机，在变量选择中大幅降低错误发现率，为统计推断与实际显著性判断提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Classical likelihood-ratio tests and $Δ$AIC exacerbate the statistical significance crisis by scaling with sample size, often flagging negligible improvements as highly significant. While causal estimands like the average treatment effect (ATE) quantify practical magnitude, their reliance on the expectation operator ties them to the data's original coordinate scale. Furthermore, existing pseudo-$R^2$ metrics are inadequate: variance-based measures ignore higher-order distributional changes, and current formulations lack invariance to monotone transformations.
  We resolve these limitations by introducing Entropic Variance (EV) as a rigorous, scale-independent generalization of error variance in ordinary least squares. We define the population EV-based parameter, $ρ^2_V$, which projects unbounded cross-entropy onto a standardized $[0,1]$ scale, and establish that the EV-based $F_\text{V}$ statistic asymptotically follows an $F$-distribution. Building on these distributional properties, we propose two estimators: the empirical population $R^2_{\text{SV}}$ and the out-of-sample predictive $R^2_{\text{SVP}}$. Both are derived by exponentiating per-observation cross-entropy and incorporate a degrees-of-freedom correction for training optimism. Leveraging the $F_\text{V}$-distribution, we derive refined $p$-values and confidence intervals for $ρ^2_V$ without requiring intractable Fisher information matrices.
  Simulation studies and a Parkinson's disease microbiome application demonstrate the superiority of variable selection via these EV-$R^2$ metrics. Notably, evaluating the $R^2_{\text{SVP}}$ of a LASSO path via data-splitting reduced false discovery rates from 80% to 6% in simulations while fully preserving signal recall.

</details>


### [215] [A Novel Tool for Evaluating Effect Modification in Older Adults with ADRD Using Medicare Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06654)
*Yilin Zhang,Michelle Shardell,Jason Falvey,Rozalina McCoy,Elizabeth Stuart,Chixiang Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于伪数据的稳健分析策略（PD-Robust），用于处理死亡截断问题，评估髋部骨折住院期间医院获得性疾病（HAC）对阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆老年患者出院后康复轨迹的影响。研究表明，85岁以下男性是高危亚群，HAC可导致其出院后居家天数减少多达23天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用真实世界数据进行比较效果研究时，受试者死亡会导致结局被截断，传统分析方法难以恰当处理。尤其对于髋部骨折且合并阿尔茨海默病及相关痴呆的老年人群，出院后死亡率高，死亡截断问题尤为突出。此外，需探索HAC效应在不同患者亚群间的异质性。现有方法在可解释性和稳健性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出PD-Robust分析策略：基于主分层（principal stratification）和主可忽略性假设下的可解释估计目标，结合结构工作模型。核心创新在于采用伪数据方法实现稳健推断，提供模型诊断和偏离假设的稳健性检验，并能够刻画主分层内患者特征。同时开发了配套R包及详细使用指南。

Result: 在Medicare理赔数据中（以出院后6个月居家天数DAH为结局），PD-Robust揭示HAC效应存在显著异质性：85岁以下男性为高风险亚组，发生HAC者比未发生HAC者平均减少多达23天居家天数，远超临床意义阈值（8天）。模拟研究进一步证实该方法估计偏倚低、统计推断准确。

Conclusion: PD-Robust策略能有效处理死亡截断问题，提供稳健的因果效应估计，并揭示效应异质性。该方法在真实世界数据中具有良好的应用前景，可用于指导针对高风险亚群的精准干预。

Abstract: Studying consequences following baseline exposures has become increasingly important for advancing comparative effectiveness research using real-world data. This case study evaluates the impact of hospital-acquired conditions (HAC) during hospitalization for hip fracture on post-discharge recovery trajectories among older adults living with Alzheimer Disease and Related Dementia, a population particularly vulnerable to high post-hospital mortality. To appropriately account for truncation of recovery trajectory due to death and to explore heterogeneity in effect modification by patient demographics, we introduce a novel pseudo data-based robust (PD-Robust) analysis strategy, accompanied by an R package and detailed usage guidance to inform real data analysis. Grounded in an interpretable estimand via principal stratification under principal ignorability and a structural working model, PD-Robust accommodates truncation by death, provides model diagnosis and robustness check against assumption violation, and facilitates the characterization of patient profiles among the principal stratum. Applied to Medicare claims data, where better recovery is defined as more days at home (DAH) over six months post-discharge, PD-Robust reveals heterogeneity in HAC effects, with males under the age of 85 years as a high-risk subgroup experiencing up to 23 fewer DAH, comparing HAC to no HAC. This exceeds the 8-day threshold regarded as clinically meaningful difference in DAH due to any exposure. Moreover, simulation studies further demonstrate that PD-Robust achieves low estimation bias and accurate statistical inference, supporting its utility in real-world data applications.

</details>


### [216] [Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Symmetric Matrix Variate Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06658)
*Malcolm Wolff,Adrian Dobra,Anton H. Westveld,Grace S. Chiu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出对称矩阵变量正态混合模型（STRUCTURED），利用个体GPS数据和社会人口学特征估计人口学因素如何影响人类活动网络。模型利用对称矩阵变量正态分布的交换性约束，将列精度矩阵参数化为行精度矩阵的多项式，大幅减少参数空间。作者开发了两个变体：STRUCTURED-FP（估计完整多项式）和STRUCTURED-RJ（结合可逆跳MCMC进行降阶选择）。模拟显示STRUCTURED-RJ在稀疏数据下优于现有方法，STRUCTURED-FP适用于大样本。应用至华盛顿州King县293人的GPS社会矩阵，发现局部犯罪环境和青年就业密度是解释每周活动重叠模式变异的主要人口学因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体活动网络的统计推断长期受限于两个挑战：缺乏适当粒度的个体数据，以及对个体移动模式建模的复杂性。GPS设备数据的普及和详细人口学信息的可获取，使研究者能够首次在精细尺度上研究人口学特征如何影响人类活动网络的空间重叠模式，但这一领域仍缺乏合适的统计模型。

Method: 提出对称矩阵变量正态混合模型（STRUCTURED），使用社会矩阵（sociomatrices）捕捉个体间随时间的概率性空间重叠。核心创新在于利用对称矩阵变量正态分布的交换性约束，将列精度矩阵参数化为行精度矩阵的多项式函数，使有效参数空间降低一个数量级。开发两个变体：STRUCTURED-FP估计完整多项式；STRUCTURED-RJ使用可逆跳MCMC（reversible-jump MCMC）进行降阶参数选择。

Result: 模拟研究表明，STRUCTURED-RJ在稀疏数据情境下优于现有方法，而STRUCTURED-FP在大样本量时表现更佳。将模型应用于华盛顿州King县293名个体的GPS衍生社会矩阵，发现局部犯罪环境和青年就业密度是解释每周活动重叠模式变异的最主要人口学因素。

Conclusion: STRUCTURED模型有效克服了个体活动网络建模的高维参数挑战，为利用大规模GPS数据研究人口学特征与人类移动模式之间的关系提供了新的统计框架。实证发现强调了社区安全和经济机会在塑造人类空间交互中的关键作用。

Abstract: Statistical inference on individual activity networks has been a historically difficult task due to the lack of available data at the appropriate granularity and the complexity of modeling individual mobility patterns. The recent availability of GPS data from individual devices, combined with highly detailed demographic information, suggests that one of these challenges can now be addressed. We introduce a new model which we call the Symmetric Matrix-Variate Normal Mixture Model (STRUCTURED) to estimate how demographic traits influence changes in human activity networks, using sociomatrices that capture the probabilistic spatial overlap between individuals over time. We exploit the commutativity constraint inherent in the symmetric matrix-variate normal distribution to parameterize the column precision matrix as a polynomial of the row precision matrix, reducing the effective parameter space by an order of magnitude. We develop two variants of STRUCTURED: STRUCTURED-FP, which estimates the full polynomial, and STRUCTURED-RJ, which uses reversible-jump MCMC to select a reduced-order parameterization. Simulation studies demonstrate that STRUCTURED-RJ outperforms existing methods in sparse-data regimes, whereas STRUCTURED-FP is preferred when sample sizes are large. We apply the model to GPS-derived sociomatrices of 293 individuals in King County, WA, finding that local crime environments and youth employment density are the dominant demographic factors explaining variation in weekly activity overlap patterns.

</details>


### [217] [Latent space models for networks with nodal multiplicative effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06676)
*Carlos Nosa,Juan Sosa*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对经典潜空间模型的推广，通过引入节点乘性效应来近似度规的共形变形，在不改变全局参考几何的前提下捕获额外的结构异质性，并开发了基于层次贝叶斯框架的优化推断方法，在多个真实网络中展现出更优的生成灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典潜空间模型通常采用固定的全局度规（如欧氏、球面或双曲度规），这限制了模型捕捉真实网络中局部结构异质性的能力。作者希望在不牺牲模型几何可解释性和逻辑预测器的前提下，通过局部度规变形来增强模型的表达能力和生成灵活性。

Method: 作者将经典潜空间模型推广为引入节点乘性效应（nodal multiplicative effects），其动机来源于潜度规的局部变形，实质上近似了度规张量的共形变形。模型保持了逻辑预测器形式和几何可解释性。推断方面，采用基于层次贝叶斯公式的优化推断方案，并引入参数正则化。

Result: 仿真实验表明所提模型具有良好的生成行为。在八个真实网络上的应用显示，该方法相比经典潜空间模型具有更强的生成灵活性，能够更准确地重现真实网络中观察到的多种拓扑属性。

Conclusion: 通过节点乘性效应近似共形度规变形的策略，成功地在保持全局参考几何和模型可解释性的同时，显著提升了潜空间模型的生成灵活性和对真实网络拓扑的拟合能力。

Abstract: Latent space models represent network nodes as points in a geometric space, with connection probabilities determined by distances between latent positions under a fixed metric, typically Euclidean, spherical, or hyperbolic. We generalize the classical formulation by introducing nodal multiplicative effects motivated by a local deformation of the latent metric. This modification approximates a conformal deformation of the metric tensor while preserving the logistic predictor and the geometric interpretability of the model, thereby capturing additional structural heterogeneity without altering the global reference geometry. We study the generative behavior of the proposed model through simulation experiments and develop an optimization-based inference scheme derived from a hierarchical Bayesian formulation with parameter regularization. Applications to eight real networks show that the proposed approach increases the generative flexibility of classical latent space models and more accurately reproduces several topological properties observed in real networks.

</details>


### [218] [Self-Supervised Representation Learning for Longitudinal Polypharmacy Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06743)
*Yunshan Duan,Eero Korpela,Yanxun Xu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出LOPEL框架，一个两阶段自监督学习方法，用于从稀疏、不规则采样的纵向用药数据中学习低维嵌入表示，并在HIV老龄化队列中验证其能识别临床上有意义的亚组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老龄化人群中多重用药现象日益普遍，尤其在接受长期治疗的HIV感染者（PWH）中，理解用药轨迹如何演变及如何表征多病共存模式至关重要。然而，纵向用药数据具有高维稀疏、观测时间不规则、药物间存在药理学结构关系等挑战，现有方法缺乏统一的通用表征学习框架。

Method: 提出LOPEL（LOngitudinal Polypharmacy Embedding Learning）两阶段自监督框架：第一阶段利用高斯过程模型融合ATC（解剖-治疗-化学）分类层次的药理学相似性，学习每次就诊的用药嵌入；第二阶段通过建模轨迹随时间的变化，利用基于Wasserstein距离的相似性表征捕捉时间动态和不确定性，构建受试者层面的嵌入。

Result: 模拟研究表明，LOPEL在高维稀疏和不规则采样等现实条件下能准确恢复潜在结构。在HIV感染者老龄化队列的应用中，LOPEL识别出在用药时机、组成和进展方面存在差异的临床有意义亚组，揭示了与风险分层和临床管理相关的异质性。

Conclusion: LOPEL为纵向多重用药数据提供了统一的表征学习框架，能有效整合药理学结构信息和时间动态特征，在HIV老龄化人群中识别出具有临床意义的异质亚组，为风险分层和个体化管理提供了新工具。

Abstract: Polypharmacy, commonly defined as the concurrent use of multiple medications, is increasingly prevalent in aging populations and is associated with adverse health outcomes. Motivated by longitudinal studies of aging people with HIV (PWH), we study how medication use evolves over time and characterizes multimorbidity patterns. Longitudinal medication data present substantial methodological challenges, including high dimensionality, sparsity, irregular observation times, and structured pharmacologic relationships among medications. Existing approaches are typically task-specific and lack a unified framework for learning general-purpose representations of medication trajectories. We propose LOPEL (LOngitudinal Polypharmacy Embedding Learning), a two-stage self-supervised framework for learning low-dimensional representations of longitudinal medication data. In the first stage, visit-level embeddings are learned using a Gaussian process model that incorporates pharmacologic similarity through the Anatomical Therapeutic Chemical hierarchy. In the second stage, subject-level embeddings are constructed by modeling trajectories over time and defining similarity through a Wasserstein-based representation capturing temporal dynamics and uncertainty. Simulation studies demonstrate that LOPEL accurately recovers latent structure under realistic conditions with high-dimensional sparsity and irregular sampling. In an application to aging cohorts of PWH, LOPEL identifies clinically meaningful subgroups that differ in the timing, composition, and progression of medication use, highlighting heterogeneity relevant for risk stratification and clinical management.

</details>


### [219] [Bridging Balancing Weights and Augmentation in Covariate-adjusted Analyses with Time-to-Event Endpoints: Theory and Practical Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06774)
*Baoshan Zhang,Yi Chen,Yu Du,Tuo Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文建立了生存分析中协变量调整的统一框架，证明平衡加权（包括校准加权和倾向性评分加权）与增强对数秩检验在一阶意义上等价，从而继承其有效性和效率增益保证，并通过模拟和REWIND试验数据加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机临床试验中，协变量调整可提高治疗效应分析的效率，但对于时间-事件终点，现有方法（增强法和加权法）各自通过不同路径达到对数秩检验和边际风险比这两个边际目标。目前缺乏一个统一框架来桥接这些方法，并明确平衡加权在生存分析中的理论性质。

Method: 作者首先将平衡加权方法拓展至时间-事件终点，涵盖校准加权（稳定平衡加权、熵平衡）和倾向性评分加权；然后从理论上证明任意满足平衡正则条件的加权方案均与增强对数秩得分及边际Cox得分的根在一阶意义上等价；最后通过模拟研究和REWIND心血管试验的实际数据分析进行验证，并对方差估计中的有限样本一类错误膨胀问题给出建议。

Result: 三种路径（增强法、平衡加权法、边际Cox得分法）在一阶意义上收敛于同一估计量；校准加权无需拟合任何模型即可达到该目标；效率增益随协变量预后强度的增强而增大；有限样本下方差估计可能导致一类错误膨胀，需谨慎处理。

Conclusion: 平衡加权方法为时间-事件终点的协变量调整提供了一条无需模型拟合的有效路径，统一了增强法和加权法的理论框架，但实际应用中需关注方差估计的有限样本表现以确保统计推断的可靠性。

Abstract: Covariate adjustment improves the efficiency of treatment-effect analyses in randomized clinical trials, provided the adjustment targets the correct quantity. For time-to-event endpoints, two marginal targets are of primary interest: the log-rank test for the presence of a treatment effect and the marginal hazard ratio for its magnitude. Existing covariate adjustment approaches reach these targets by different ways. Augmentation adjusts the log-rank score by regressing derived outcomes on the baseline covariates within each arm. Weighting instead reweights the two arms to balance the covariates before the survival comparison is formed: inverse probability weighting does so through a fitted propensity model, while calibration weighting solves directly for weights that match covariate means. In this manuscript, we first develop balancing weighting for time-to-event endpoints, covering both calibration weights (stable balancing weights and entropy balancing) and propensity score weights, and prove that any balancing-regular weighting is first-order equivalent to the augmented log-rank score and to the root of the marginal Cox score. All three routes therefore deliver the same estimator to first order, and calibration reaches it without fitting any model. The weighted procedures thereby inherit the validity and guaranteed efficiency gain of the augmentation approach. In addition, we show that the efficiency gain grows with the prognostic strength of the adjustment covariates, while the practical caveat lies in variance estimation, for which we give recommendations to guard against finite-sample Type I error inflation. We further confirm our results through simulation studies and an analysis of the REWIND cardiovascular trial.

</details>


### [220] [A Continuous-Weighted Win Ratio for Hierarchical Composite Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07184)
*Kexuan Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续加权赢率比(continuous-weighted win ratio)方法，通过平滑权重取代分层复合终点中各级别间的硬性转换，使低优先级终点可在高优先级终点接近平局时逐步贡献信息。该方法包含严格赢率比和硬阈值规则作为特例或极限情形，并在治疗效果集中于低优先级终点时可提高检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分层复合终点的严格层级规则要求仅在所有高优先级终点均平局或不可评估时才使用低优先级终点，这导致当治疗效果主要通过低优先级终点表达时统计功效下降。现有基于阈值的扩展虽有所缓解，但权重转换仍为硬性。需一种更灵活的加权机制以更好捕捉治疗效应。

Method: 提出连续加权赢率比：用平滑权重函数替代终点层级间的硬性过渡，使低优先级终点在高优先级终点接近平局时渐进贡献。所得估计量为双样本U统计量，利用Hoeffding投影导出大样本分布，并提供一致性的插件方差估计量。通过局部备择假设和局部效率阐释调节参数的作用。

Result: 模拟研究表明：当治疗效果集中在低优先级终点时，放宽严格层级可提升检验功效；但当最高优先级终点承载主要治疗信号时，可能降低功效。严格赢率比和硬阈值规则均为所提方法的特例或极限情形。

Conclusion: 连续加权赢率比为分层复合终点的分析提供了一种统一且灵活的框架，通过调节平滑参数可在不同治疗效应分布场景下权衡功效表现。

Abstract: Hierarchical composite endpoints are commonly used in clinical trials when component out- comes differ in clinical importance. The win ratio compares patients across treatment groups according to a pre-specified order of clinical priority and has an intuitive interpretation. A strict hierarchical rule, however, uses a lower-priority endpoint only when all higher-priority endpoints are tied or non-informative. This may reduce power when the treatment effect is mainly ex- pressed through lower-priority outcomes. Recent threshold-based extensions relax this hierar- chy by allowing lower-priority outcomes to contribute when higher-priority outcomes are within pre-specified margins. We propose a continuous-weighted win ratio for hierarchical composite endpoints. The method replaces hard transitions between endpoint levels with smooth weights, so that a lower-priority endpoint can contribute gradually when the higher-priority endpoint is close to tied. The resulting estimator is a two-sample U-statistic. We derive its large-sample distribution using the Hoeffding projection and provide a consistent plug-in variance estimator. We also describe the role of the tuning parameter through local alternatives and local efficiency. The proposed class includes the strict win ratio and hard-threshold rules as special or limit- ing cases. Simulation studies show that relaxing the strict hierarchy can improve power when treatment effects are concentrated on lower-priority endpoints, but may reduce power when the highest-priority endpoint carries the main treatment signal.

</details>


### [221] [Recovering Latent Structure in Massive Datasets: A PCA Study of 10 Billion and 1 Trillion Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07334)
*Mike Crowhurst*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析百亿级和万亿级观测数据集，实证检验了PCA在超大样本下的收敛行为。随机数据集的结果表明PCA解在极大样本下高度稳定，而工程化数据集则成功恢复了预设的三因子潜在结构，表明PCA在达到万亿级别之前即可能已实现实用收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管理论上样本估计量会随样本量增大而收敛于总体值，但关于PCA在数十亿乃至万亿观测规模下的实际行为，目前缺乏实证证据。该研究旨在填补这一空白，验证PCA在极端大规模数据下的稳定性和收敛特性。

Method: 研究采用三个数据集进行对比分析：(1) 100亿观测的随机数据集；(2) 1万亿观测的随机数据集；(3) 100亿观测的工程化数据集（预设三个潜因子）。通过比较各数据集下PCA解的差异，评估样本量对解稳定性的影响及潜因子恢复能力。

Result: 两个随机数据集的PCA解几乎完全一致，表明超大样本下PCA具有显著稳定性。工程化数据集成功提取出三个主导主成分，合计解释99.996%的标准化总方差，并准确恢复了预设的潜因子结构。

Conclusion: PCA解在大样本下可快速收敛，且在实际应用中可能在远低于万亿样本量时即已达到实用收敛水平。这一发现对遥感、数字制图、环境建模等需处理百万至数十亿观测数据的领域具有重要意义。

Abstract: This study investigated the behavior of Principal Component Analysis (PCA) when applied to datasets with extremely large numbers of observations. Although statistical theory suggests that sampling error diminishes and sample estimates converge toward their population values as sample size increases, relatively little empirical evidence exists regarding the behavior of PCA at scales measured in billions or trillions of observations. Three datasets were analyzed: a 10-billion observation random dataset, a 1-trillion observation random dataset, and a 10-billion observation engineered dataset designed to contain three latent factors.
  Results showed that the PCA solutions obtained from the 10BillionRandom and 1TrillionRandom datasets were nearly identical, indicating substantial stability of PCA at extremely large sample sizes. In contrast, the engineered dataset produced three dominant principal components that accounted for 99.996% of the total standardized variance and successfully recovered the intended latent-factor structure. These findings suggest that PCA solutions converge rapidly at very large sample sizes and suggest that PCA solutions may reach practical convergence well before sample sizes reach the trillions. These findings have implications for large-scale applications in fields such as remote sensing, digital mapping, environmental modeling, and other domains where datasets routinely contain millions or billions of observations.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Mixture of Geodesic Factor Analyzers on Riemannian Homogeneous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06971)
*Hengchao Chen,Yuanyao Tan,Chao Huang,Hongtu Zhu,Qiang Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出黎曼齐次空间上的测地因子分析混合模型（MGFA），在各混合成分内采用测地因子模型，能够聚类具有各向异性子总体的流形值数据。作者证明了MGFA最大似然估计的根-n一致性，填补了黎曼径向分布混合模型的理论空白，并通过数值实验和形状分析案例验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼流形上的混合模型（如黎曼径向分布混合）难以刻画各向异性的子总体结构，缺乏对高维流形数据的灵活聚类能力，且相关最大似然估计的渐近理论尚不完善。

Method: 在黎曼齐次空间上构建测地因子分析混合模型（MGFA），每个混合成分内部使用测地因子模型以捕捉各向异性；证明MLE的根-n一致性；提出迭代估计算法，并在球面、形状空间和双曲空间上实现。

Result: 数值实验表明，MGFA在模型设定正确时显著优于竞争方法，在模型误设下仍保持稳健。胼胝体和左侧海马体形状数据集的案例研究验证了其在2D轮廓和3D形状分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: MGFA为流形值数据的聚类提供了一种兼具表达力与理论保证的解决方案，在多个黎曼空间和实际形状分析任务中均表现出色。

Abstract: This paper introduces Mixtures of Geodesic Factor Analyzers (MGFA) on Riemannian homogeneous spaces. MGFA uses a geodesic factor model within each mixture component, providing greater expressiveness than mixtures of Riemannian radial distributions and enabling clustering of manifold-valued data with anisotropic subpopulations. We establish root-$n$ consistency for the MGFA maximum likelihood estimator (MLE), thereby filling a theoretical gap for mixtures of Riemannian radial distributions as a special case. We also propose an iterative estimation algorithm and implement it on spheres, shape spaces, and hyperbolic spaces. Numerical experiments show that MGFA substantially outperforms competing methods in well-specified regimes while remaining robust under model misspecification. Finally, case studies on corpus callosum and left hippocampus shape datasets demonstrate MGFA's effectiveness for both 2D contour and 3D shape analysis.

</details>


### [223] [Optimized Certainty Equivalent Risk Minimization Using Samples: Algorithms, Convergence Rates, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07113)
*Sumedh Gupte,Prashanth L. A.,Sanjay P. Bhat*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了优化确定性等价(OCE)风险的优化问题，建立了OCE与基于效用的短缺风险(UBSR)之间的联系，据此提出了OCE估计量及其梯度估计量，推导了均方误差的非渐近界，并将其嵌入随机梯度算法以优化OCE，给出了收敛速率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCE是一类广泛应用的风险度量（涵盖熵风险、均值-方差风险、CVaR的光滑变体等），但其在无界随机变量下的推广、估计以及随机梯度优化方面缺乏系统性的理论支撑。作者旨在填补这一空白，为OCE的估计与优化提供严格的统计和计算保证。

Method: 首先，作者给出OCE与UBSR之间的特征化联系，使得OCE可通过UBSR的样本平均近似(SAA)来估计，并推导了OCE估计量的MSE界。其次，利用该联系导出OCE梯度的表达式，构建梯度估计量并给出其MSE的非渐近界。最后，将该梯度估计量嵌入随机梯度(SG)算法，推导了优化收敛的非渐近界，并通过三个实验验证了方法在投资组合优化和不确定性量化中的有效性。

Result: 推导了OCE估计量和OCE梯度估计量的均方误差非渐近界；为随机梯度算法优化OCE提供了非渐近收敛速率界；实验结果表明所提算法在投资组合优化和不确定性量化任务中表现有效。

Conclusion: 通过建立OCE与UBSR的对偶联系，作者为OCE的估计与随机梯度优化提供了系统性的理论框架，给出了具有非渐近保证的估计量和优化算法，并验证了其在金融和机器学习应用中的实用性。

Abstract: We consider the optimization of the Optimized Certainty Equivalent (OCE) risk, with applications including portfolio optimization in finance, and uncertainty quantification, classification, and regression in machine learning. Our contributions cover popular special cases of OCE, such as entropic risk, mean-variance risk, and smooth variants of Conditional Value-at-Risk. Our treatment sets out the conditions that facilitate the extension of OCE to unbounded r.v.s.. We provide a useful characterization of OCE that links OCE to utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR). Our characterization enables us to form an OCE estimator from the classic sample-average approximation (SAA) of UBSR. We derive mean-squared error (MSE) bounds for our proposed OCE estimator. For OCE optimization, we first derive an expression for the OCE gradient using the characterization linking OCE to UBSR. This expression serves as the basis for a gradient estimator for the OCE. We derive non-asymptotic bounds on the MSE for the proposed OCE gradient estimator. We incorporate the aforementioned gradient estimator into a stochastic gradient (SG) algorithm to optimize OCE and quantify its convergence rate using non-asymptotic bounds that we derive. Finally, we present three experiments that use our OCE optimization algorithm to solve portfolio optimization and uncertainty quantification problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向跨域多标签节点分类的多语义基图基础模型（MSB-GFM），通过将多标签节点建模为语义基的自适应组合来解决传统图基础模型中单标签假设导致的语义纠缠问题，并结合语义-结构双通道架构与域对抗训练实现跨域知识迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型（GFMs）基于单标签假设，将多标签节点嵌入为单一表示，导致多个语义在表示空间中纠缠，难以同时区分多个标签。此外，现有多标签节点分类方法局限于域内场景，缺乏跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出MSB-GFM框架：1）多语义基表示学习范式——将每个多标签节点建模为多个语义基的自适应组合，赋予模型灵活的多语义建模能力；2）语义-结构双通道架构，结合域对抗训练，实现有效的跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提模型在多标签节点分类任务上的有效性，尤其在跨域场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基表示学习有效解耦多标签语义，结合双通道架构与域对抗训练实现了跨域多标签节点分类，突破了现有图基础模型的单标签限制与域内泛化瓶颈。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [225] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EntropyMoE，一种基于动态字节块的MoE架构，利用patch熵作为路由信号实现稀疏条件计算，在bits-per-byte指标上优于密集和稀疏基线，同时保持下游任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字节级大语言模型对所有patch应用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据patch的语义和粒度差异自适应调整模型容量，导致计算资源分配不够高效。

Method: 设计EntropyMoE架构：(1)用Top-K专家层替换全局patch Transformer中的密集前馈模块；(2)以每个动态patch作为专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖量决定工作负载核算；(3)直接从patch熵中选择专家，使路由信号与动态patch构建的粒度信号一致；(4)patch熵与长度共同定义专家特化的特征空间。

Result: EntropyMoE在held-out bits-per-byte指标上优于所有匹配的密集和稀疏基线模型，同时下游任务准确率保持可比水平。

Conclusion: Patch熵是稀疏条件计算中有效的路由坐标，成功将MoE建模从基于tokenizer的表征扩展到字节级表示。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [226] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）方法，利用选择-拒绝响应对的贡献对比来忠实解释稀疏MoE奖励模型中专家的行为，相比基于路由、分数和稀疏自编码器的方法，CoCo提供更连贯、忠实且专业化的解释，同时保持竞争力强的奖励建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE奖励模型中，现有的基于路由权重的专家解释方法只能揭示专家"接收"了哪些提示，却无法揭示专家如何"判断"响应，只提供了专家行为的部分描述。因此需要一种能联合捕捉路由行为和偏好判断行为的响应级解释方法。

Method: 提出CoCo（Contribution-Contrast）响应级解释方法，通过识别具有最大贡献对比的选择-拒绝响应对来忠实刻画专家的角色，该对比度量同时捕获了专家的路由行为和偏好判断行为。并通过自动评估和人工评估进行验证。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，CoCo相比基于路由权重、基于分数和基于稀疏自编码器的替代方法，能产出更连贯、更忠实、更专业化的专家解释，同时保持具有竞争力的奖励建模准确度。

Conclusion: 这是首个对MoE奖励模型解释方法的系统研究，CoCo弥补了现有方法仅关注路由行为而忽略偏好判断行为的不足，为理解和解释MoE奖励模型中的专家角色提供了更全面的方案。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [227] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUCID是一种基于大语言模型引导的无监督社区检测方法，受自然系统相变动力学启发，通过四阶段流水线（局部初始化、多因素合并、多粒度精炼、全局选择）实现可解释且无需训练的社区发现，在多个真实数据集上达到了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标驱动型社区检测方法难以处理复杂图结构，深度学习方法虽提升性能但缺乏可解释性且依赖标注数据与训练。为充分利用LLM的强推理能力和世界知识，实现可解释、无标签的社区检测，作者提出了LUCID。

Method: LUCID受自然系统相变动力学启发，设计为四阶段流水线：(1) 局部视图社区初始化：利用k-ego上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构；(2) 多因素社区合并：使用LLM诱导的形式化规则迭代合并局部社区；(3) 多粒度社区精炼：并行应用LLM诱导的由粗到细规则减少边界噪声；(4) 全局视图社区选择：基于拓扑紧凑性和边界清晰度筛选高质量社区。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID作为一种无监督方法，达到了最先进性能，并一致性地超越了主流的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID成功将LLM的世界知识和推理能力融入社区检测，通过相变动力学启发的四阶段设计，在不依赖标注数据和训练的情况下实现了可解释且高性能的社区发现。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [228] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADIAS 提出以问题为中心的自动化 Agent 设计框架，通过持久化问题状态替代传统以候选为中心的优化方式，在五个交互基准上平均提升 25.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化 Agent 设计方法以候选 agent 为中心组织跨轮经验，修复进度隐式化，导致修复定位低效、部分进展难以整合、无效干预跨轮传播等问题。

Method: 提出问题为中心的 Agent 优化范式，并实现 ADIAS 框架：包含两个核心机制——(1) 持久化问题状态，维护问题身份、生命周期状态、支撑证据及干预-结果历史；(2) 问题引导优化，利用该状态联合提出修复目标和修订方向，进行聚焦的全代码修改。

Result: ADIAS 在五个交互基准上平均超越最强基线 25.2%，在四个骨干模型上均取得一致提升。消融实验显示，移除持久化问题状态或将问题导向修订替换为候选导向策略，性能下降最高达 40.7%。

Conclusion: 以问题为中心的优化通过显式持久化问题状态有效解决了候选中心方法的固有缺陷，显著提升了自动化 Agent 设计的效率和效果。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [229] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAP（Middle-layer Attention Prediction）提出了一种新的视觉 token 剪枝方法：通过问题对比教师选择机制为每个样本动态定位最优中间层，并将其注意力蒸馏到轻量预测器中，在首个语言模型层之前完成剪枝，无需实际计算注意力图。在 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 上仅保留 5.56% 视觉 token 即可保持 97.5% 性能，实现 3.09 倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于中间层文本到视觉注意力的剪枝方法存在两个问题：(1) 对问题最敏感的中间层因样本而异，固定层是次优的；(2) 获取该层注意力仍需先经过多个语言模型层处理大量视觉 token，此时大量计算已被消耗，削弱了剪枝的加速收益。

Method: 提出 MAP 方法：(1) Question Contrastive Teacher Selection——通过对比原始问题和参考问题下的注意力分布，为每个样本动态识别最优教师层；(2) 将选定层的注意力知识蒸馏到轻量预测器中，该预测器直接从多模态输入特征估计视觉 token 重要性；(3) 推理时，结合预测的重要性分数与多样性准则，在首个语言模型层之前完成剪枝，无需实际计算任何注意力图，且与现有推理加速技术兼容。

Result: 在 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 的十个基准测试上，MAP 仅使用 5.56% 的视觉 token 即可保留未剪枝模型 97.5% 的性能，实现 3.09 倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: MAP 通过样本自适应教师层选择与注意力蒸馏，实现了在语言模型处理之前高效、准确的视觉 token 剪枝，显著降低了 MLLM 的计算开销同时保持性能，展示了注意力知识蒸馏在模型加速中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [230] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebGrader 是一个自进化的程序化评分器，通过为每个网站请求自动生成可执行的 Flow Contract 作为 RL 奖励信号，解决了网站生成任务中奖励设计瓶颈。它分离了测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集和语义判断，并在 WebGen-Bench 上将 8B 策略训练至 52.01% 的功能成功率，超越多个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于强化学习训练大模型生成完整网站面临奖励设计瓶颈：手工编写浏览器脚本可执行但成本高、难以覆盖开放式需求；VLM 和 GUI-agent 评分器虽可扩展，但可能在观察到决定性状态前就给出判断，导致奖励信号不可靠。

Method: WebGrader 采用四阶段分离架构：(1) 从网站需求自动推导交互流程并表示为可执行的 Flow Contract；(2) 在真实浏览器中实例化生成的项目，基于源码和实时 DOM 进行动作锚定，沿浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久化状态证据；(3) 通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用验证技能，在分离的验证页面上筛选，冻结技能图后用于策略训练；(4) 仅在观察到所请求的状态转换后才发出 Pass 判定。

Result: 在 WebGen-Bench 上，WebGrader 训练的 8B 策略达到 52.01% 功能成功率，比匹配的外观+脚本奖励高出 7.88 个百分点，并超越 o4-mini 和 DeepSeek-v4-flash。在 WG-core-250 上，策略达到 44.953 的 Full Score，超越 Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: WebGrader 通过将测试规划、动作锚定、证据收集与语义判断解耦，并利用自进化技能发现机制，为 RL 训练提供了更可靠、可扩展的程序化奖励，显著提升了 LLM 生成完整网站的功能性，展现出超越更大规模模型的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [231] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: C4 是一个受认知启发的评估框架，用于衡量多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）基于成语的跨概念创造力。它通过构建跨概念网络中的“桥路径”来编码和解码概念关系，并发布了包含多种任务设置的 C4-Eval 评估集。对十个 MLLMs 的测试表明，即使最强模型准确率也仅约 50%，揭示了当前模型在创造性跨概念理解上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs 的创造性能力在设计、教育和人机协作中至关重要，但由于缺乏显式目标和奖励信号，评估难度大。现有基准多关注准确性任务，忽视了基于跨概念理解的“接受性创造力”。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一种可操作、结构化的评估范式。

Method: 作者提出 C4 框架，将评估分为编码（将目标槽映射到可图像化的替代概念，沿跨概念网络中的桥路径）和解码（模型推理恢复原意）。核心是人工标注并经第三方审阅的跨概念网络，难度由桥数量和深度索引。基于此构建 C4-Eval，包含 184 个合成项和 37 个人工收集的成语图，实例化为五种任务设置，共 884 个答案恢复案例。

Result: 在十个 MLLMs 上评估，最强闭源模型主准确率仅为 50.7% 和 48.0%，开源模型更低。候选约束能大幅提升准确率，但桥提示和解释请求仅带来微弱增益。结果暴露了当前 MLLMs 在通过跨概念关系解码创造性编码意义方面存在重大差距。

Conclusion: 当前 MLLMs 在跨概念创造性理解方面能力有限，C4 框架为此提供了可靠且可扩展的评估工具。未来需进一步提升模型对非显式概念关系的推理能力，以缩小与人类创造力理解之间的差距。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks.
